# Layer changelog


Below you will find information on schema changes. For more details on the use of layers, see [Invoking API methods](/api/invoking.md#layers).

### [Layer 225](/schema?layer=225)

To view all changes since the last documentation refresh, start reading at [layer 215](#layer-215).

This documentation release includes the following new and substantially overhauled articles, documenting pre-existing features:

- [Group calls, video chats, live stories, conferences and livestreams »](/api/group-calls.md)
- [One-to-one calls »](/api/calls.md)
- [Completely overhauled the E2E conference documentation »](/api/end-to-end/group-calls.md)
- [Completely overhauled the file reference database schema, making it significantly easier to autogenerate the file reference database »](/api/file-references.md)
- [Applying basic group updates »](/api/peers.md#basic-group-updates)
- [Applying group call updates »](/api/end-to-end/group-calls.md#handling-updates)
- [Message ID sequences »](/api/updates.md#message-id-sequences)
- [More detailed instructions for update gap handling (also considering gaps beyond the max message box size) »](/api/updates.md#recovering-gaps-for-very-old-messages)
- [Parallel sessions »](/api/datacenter.md#parallel-sessions)
- [updateMessageID handling »](/api/updates.md#updatemessageid-updates)
- [Documented rate limits for live drafts »](/api/bots/ai.md#live-response-streaming)
- [Content protection »](/api/content-protection.md)
- [Message views »](/api/views.md)
- [Sending messages on behalf of owned channels and groups »](/api/channel.md#sending-messages-on-behalf-of-owned-channels-and-groups)
- [Working with basic group participants »](/api/channel.md#basic-group-participants)
- [Leaving groups/channels (full flow) »](/api/channel.md#leaving-groups-channels)
- [Added the layer number to the RPC error DB »](/api/errors.md)
- [Improved the takeout documentation »](/api/takeout.md)
- Documented the [`navigationBarColor` »](/api/web-events.md#navigationbarcolor), [`actionBarColor` »](/api/web-events.md#actionbarcolor), [`allowScroll` »](/api/web-events.md#allowscroll), [`siteName` »](/api/web-events.md#sitename) event types
- [`whitelisted_bots` »](/api/config.md#whitelisted-bots)
- Detailed changelogs for API configuration keys, web events, limits, suggested actions and other API constants will now be automatically listed in the appropriate layer changelog, instead of the latest layer.
- Published an aggregated, machine-readable index of all [API constants and push updates »](/api/config.md#machine-readable-configuration-index) at the new [/api/config.json »](/api/config.json.md) permalink.
- The [RPC error database »](/api/errors.md#error-database) is now also published as a machine-readable [/api/errors.json »](/api/errors.json.md) permalink, replacing the previous embedded file link.
- Multiple other improvements and fixes across the board.

And documenting the following new features (a brief overview of features introduced since [layer 215](#layer-215)):

- AI features: [message composition »](/api/ai.md#compose-messages), [AI compose tones »](/api/ai.md#ai-compose-tones) and [message summarization »](/api/ai.md#summarize-messages)
- Bot AI features: [live drafts »](/api/bots/ai.md#live-response-streaming)
- [Bot forums »](/api/forum.md#bot-forums)
- [Managed bots »](/api/bots/managed-bots.md)
- [Improved styled text entities »](/api/entities.md)
- [Guest mode for bots »](/api/bots/guest-mode.md)
- [Gift auctions »](/api/auctions.md)
- [Passkeys »](/api/passkeys.md)
- [Live stories »](/api/group-calls.md#live-stories), [in-call messages »](/api/group-calls.md#in-call-messages), [paid live story comments »](/api/group-calls.md#paid-live-story-comments) and [paid live story donations »](/api/group-calls.md#paid-live-story-donations)
- [Recent story summaries »](/api/stories.md#recent-story-summaries)
- [Repeating scheduled messages »](/api/scheduled-messages.md#repeating-scheduled-messages)
- [Message views »](/api/views.md#view-counters) and [read metrics »](/api/views.md#read-metrics)
- [Open-answer polls »](/api/poll.md#open-answer-polls), [unread poll votes »](/api/poll.md#unread-poll-votes), [poll answer media attachments »](/api/poll.md#sending-a-poll), [replies to poll options »](/api/poll.md#replying-to-poll-options), [poll search »](/api/poll.md#searching-for-polls) and [poll statistics »](/api/stats.md#poll-statistics)
- [Group member/admin tags »](/api/rank.md)
- [Group/channel ownership transfer »](/api/channel.md#transferring-ownership-of-a-group-channel) and [leave handoff flows »](/api/channel.md#leaving-groups-channels)
- [Date styled text entities »](/api/entities.md#date-entities) and [diff styled text entities »](/api/entities.md#diff-entities)
- [Collectible gifts »](/api/gifts.md#collectible-gifts): [crafting »](/api/gifts.md#crafting-collectible-gifts), [purchase offers »](/api/gifts.md#collectible-gift-purchase-offers), [hosted gifts »](/api/gifts.md#hosted-collectible-gifts), [collectible message palettes »](/api/colors.md#collectible-message-palettes) and [collectible profile palettes »](/api/colors.md#collectible-profile-palettes).
- [Private contact notes »](/api/contacts.md#private-notes-for-contacts)
- [Birthdays »](/api/profile.md#birthday), [music profile tab »](/api/profile.md#music), [managed bot manager info »](/api/profile.md#managed-bot-manager), [personal channels »](/api/profile.md#personal-channel) and [unofficial client warnings »](/api/profile.md#unofficial-client-warning)
- [Private chat content protection »](/api/content-protection.md#for-users)
- [Button styles »](/api/bots/buttons.md#button-styles)
- [Peer requests for Mini Apps »](/api/bots/buttons.md#requesting-peers-via-mini-apps)
- [Anonymous group admins »](/api/rights.md#anonymous-admins) can now [toggle checklist items »](/api/todo.md#mark-items-as-completed)
- [Removing participant reactions »](/api/reactions.md#removing-participant-reactions)
- [Message effects »](/api/effects.md) support for forwarded messages
- [Login email setup suggestions »](/api/auth.md#email-verification) and [passkey setup suggestions »](/api/config.md#setup-passkey)
- [OAuth login »](/api/url-authorization.md)
- [`blur_enabled` in `fullscreen_changed` events »](/api/bots/webapps.md#fullscreen-changed)
- New [deep link »](/api/links.md) types: 
    - [Parameterized settings deep links, with support for opening a specific settings page or subsection »](/api/links.md#settings-links)
    - [Contact list deep links »](/api/links.md#contacts-links)
    - [Chat creation deep links »](/api/links.md#new-chat-links)
    - [Chat list deep links »](/api/links.md#chat-list-links)
    - [Live story deep links »](/api/links.md#live-story-links)
    - [Story composer deep links »](/api/links.md#post-story-links)
    - [Timestamp links »](/api/links.md#timestamp-links)
    - [Highlighting of a specific checklist task or poll option in message deep links »](/api/links.md#message-links)

This layer introduces the following features:

- [Custom AI compose tones »](/api/ai.md#ai-compose-tones)
- [Guest mode for bots »](/api/bots/guest-mode.md)
- [Poll statistics »](/api/stats.md#poll-statistics)
- [Participant reaction moderation »](/api/reactions.md#removing-participant-reactions)
- [Allowed bots to fetch the history of the personal channel of any user »](/api/profile.md#personal-channel)

#### API constants

##### Premium limits

[Telegram Premium limit identifiers &raquo;](/api/premium.md#double-limits).

- Added [`double_limits__aicompose_tone_saved` &raquo;](/api/premium.md#double-limits-aicompose-tone-saved)

##### Web page type values

Web page type values from the [webPage](/constructor/webPage.md) constructor.

- Added `telegram_aicomposetone` - Preview of an [AI compose tone link »](/api/links.md#ai-compose-tone-links), shared when previewing a [custom AI composer tone »](/api/ai.md#ai-compose-tones). The `attributes` field contains a [webPageAttributeAiComposeTone](/constructor/webPageAttributeAiComposeTone.md) with the [custom emoji »](/api/custom-emoji.md) representing the tone; clients render a button that applies the shared tone.

#### [Schema](/schema.md) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [messages.setBotGuestChatResult](/method/messages.setBotGuestChatResult.md) - Bots may use this method to answer a [guest mode »](/api/bots/guest-mode.md) query received via an [updateBotGuestChatQuery](/constructor/updateBotGuestChatQuery.md) update, providing the message to post into the chat as a guest, see [here »](/api/bots/guest-mode.md#handling-guest-queries-bot-side) for more info.
* Added [messages.deleteParticipantReactions](/method/messages.deleteParticipantReactions.md) - As an admin, remove all of a specific participant's [reactions](/api/reactions.md) from every message in a group or channel.
* Added [messages.deleteParticipantReaction](/method/messages.deleteParticipantReaction.md) - As an admin, remove all of a specific participant's [reactions](/api/reactions.md) from a single message.
* Added [messages.getPersonalChannelHistory](/method/messages.getPersonalChannelHistory.md) - Fetch the message history of a user's [personal channel »](/api/profile.md#personal-channel).
* Added [bots.getAccessSettings](/method/bots.getAccessSettings.md) - Get the [access restriction settings »](/api/bots/managed-bots.md#managing-a-managed-bot) of a managed bot; can only be called by the manager bot.
* Added [bots.editAccessSettings](/method/bots.editAccessSettings.md) - Edit the [access restriction settings »](/api/bots/managed-bots.md#managing-a-managed-bot) of a managed bot; can only be called by the manager bot.
* Added [stats.getPollStats](/method/stats.getPollStats.md) - Get [statistics](/api/stats.md#poll-statistics) for a poll sent in a message.
* Added [aicompose.createTone](/method/aicompose.createTone.md) - Create a new custom [AI composer tone »](/api/ai.md#ai-compose-tones).
* Added [aicompose.updateTone](/method/aicompose.updateTone.md) - Edit a custom [AI composer tone »](/api/ai.md#ai-compose-tones) previously created by the current user. Only the fields whose flag is set will be modified.
* Added [aicompose.saveTone](/method/aicompose.saveTone.md) - Install or uninstall an [AI composer tone »](/api/ai.md#ai-compose-tones), adding it to or removing it from the list of saved tones of the current user.
* Added [aicompose.deleteTone](/method/aicompose.deleteTone.md) - Permanently delete a custom [AI composer tone »](/api/ai.md#ai-compose-tones) created by the current user.
* Added [aicompose.getTone](/method/aicompose.getTone.md) - Fetch information about a single [AI composer tone »](/api/ai.md#ai-compose-tones), for example to resolve a shared tone deep link.
* Added [aicompose.getTones](/method/aicompose.getTones.md) - Fetch the list of saved [AI composer tones »](/api/ai.md#ai-compose-tones) of the current user.
* Added [aicompose.getToneExample](/method/aicompose.getToneExample.md) - Fetch an example showing how an [AI composer tone »](/api/ai.md#ai-compose-tones) rephrases a sample message, used as a preview in the tone picker.

##### Changed Methods

* Added **bots_guestchat** parameter in [contacts.getTopPeers](/method/contacts.getTopPeers.md)
* Added **tone** parameter, removed **change_tone** parameter in [messages.composeMessageWithAI](/method/messages.composeMessageWithAI.md)

##### New Constructors

* Added [updateBotGuestChatQuery](/constructor/updateBotGuestChatQuery.md) - Sent to [guest bots »](/api/bots/guest-mode.md) when a user invokes the bot as a guest in a chat. The bot should reply by invoking [messages.setBotGuestChatResult](/method/messages.setBotGuestChatResult.md), see [here »](/api/bots/guest-mode.md#handling-guest-queries-bot-side) for more info.
* Added [updateAiComposeTones](/constructor/updateAiComposeTones.md) - The list of saved [AI composer tones »](/api/ai.md#ai-compose-tones) of the current user has changed, and should be refetched using [aicompose.getTones](/method/aicompose.getTones.md).
* Added [topPeerCategoryBotsGuestChat](/constructor/topPeerCategoryBotsGuestChat.md) - Top [guest bots »](/api/bots/guest-mode.md), i.e. bots most frequently invoked as guests in chats.
* Added [webPageAttributeAiComposeTone](/constructor/webPageAttributeAiComposeTone.md) - [Webpage attribute](/type/WebPageAttribute.md) attached to a [webPage](/constructor/webPage.md) of type `telegram_aicomposetone`, generated when previewing a shared [AI composer tone »](/api/ai.md#ai-compose-tones) via an [AI compose tone link »](/api/links.md#ai-compose-tone-links).
* Added [stats.pollStats](/constructor/stats.pollStats.md) - [Statistics](/api/stats.md#poll-statistics) for a poll sent in a message.
* Added [inputAiComposeToneDefault](/constructor/inputAiComposeToneDefault.md) - References a built-in, default [AI composer tone »](/api/ai.md#ai-compose-tones) by its string identifier.
* Added [inputAiComposeToneID](/constructor/inputAiComposeToneID.md) - References a custom [AI composer tone »](/api/ai.md#ai-compose-tones) by its ID and access hash.
* Added [inputAiComposeToneSlug](/constructor/inputAiComposeToneSlug.md) - References a custom [AI composer tone »](/api/ai.md#ai-compose-tones) by its public slug, used when opening an [AI compose tone link »](/api/links.md#ai-compose-tone-links).
* Added [aiComposeTone](/constructor/aiComposeTone.md) - A custom [AI composer tone »](/api/ai.md#ai-compose-tones), used to rephrase messages in a specific style with the [AI message composer](/api/ai.md#compose-messages).
* Added [aiComposeToneDefault](/constructor/aiComposeToneDefault.md) - A built-in, default [AI composer tone »](/api/ai.md#ai-compose-tones), identified by a string identifier rather than by a numeric ID.
* Added [aicompose.tonesNotModified](/constructor/aicompose.tonesNotModified.md) - The list of saved [AI composer tones »](/api/ai.md#ai-compose-tones) hasn't changed since the last time it was fetched (i.e. the `hash` passed to [aicompose.getTones](/method/aicompose.getTones.md) is still valid).
* Added [aicompose.tones](/constructor/aicompose.tones.md) - The list of saved [AI composer tones »](/api/ai.md#ai-compose-tones) of the current user.
* Added [aiComposeToneExample](/constructor/aiComposeToneExample.md) - An example showing how an [AI composer tone »](/api/ai.md#ai-compose-tones) rephrases a message, used as a preview in the tone picker.
* Added [bots.accessSettings](/constructor/bots.accessSettings.md) - Access restriction settings for a [managed bot »](/api/bots/managed-bots.md#managing-a-managed-bot).

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **bot_guestchat** parameter in [user](/constructor/user.md)
* Added **guestchat_via_from** parameter in [message](/constructor/message.md)
* Added **premium_days** parameter in [auth.sentCodePaymentRequired](/constructor/auth.sentCodePaymentRequired.md)
* Added **subscribers_only**, **countries_iso2** parameters in [poll](/constructor/poll.md)
* Added **can_view_stats** parameter in [pollResults](/constructor/pollResults.md)
* Added **send_reactions** parameter in [chatBannedRights](/constructor/chatBannedRights.md)
* Added **premium_days** parameter in [inputStorePaymentAuthCode](/constructor/inputStorePaymentAuthCode.md)

#### [Changes in the file reference database map file &raquo;](/api/file-references.md)

##### New traversers incoming

* Added <pre><code>traverseIncomingConstructor{predicate: <a href='/constructor/updateBotGuestChatQuery'>updateBotGuestChatQuery</a>, params: [message, reference_messages], type: <a href='/type/Update'>Update</a>}</code></pre>
* Added <pre><code>traverseMethodResult{name: <a href='/method/messages.getPersonalChannelHistory'>messages.getPersonalChannelHistory</a>}</code></pre>

##### New traversers outgoing

* Added <pre><code>traverseMethodCall{name: <a href='/method/messages.setBotGuestChatResult'>messages.setBotGuestChatResult</a>, params: [result]}</code></pre>

### [Layer 224](/schema?layer=224)

This layer introduces the following features:

- [AI message editor »](/api/ai.md#compose-messages)
- [Diff styled text entities »](/api/entities.md#diff-entities)
- [Message views and read metrics »](/api/views.md)
- [Managed bots »](/api/bots/managed-bots.md)
- [Managed bot manager info »](/api/profile.md#managed-bot-manager)
- [Peer requests for mini apps »](/api/bots/buttons.md#requesting-peers-via-mini-apps)
- [Detailed message metrics »](/api/views.md#read-metrics)
- [Open-answer polls »](/api/poll.md#open-answer-polls)
- [Unread poll votes »](/api/poll.md#unread-poll-votes)
- [Poll answer media attachments »](/api/poll.md#sending-a-poll)
- [Replies to poll options »](/api/poll.md#replying-to-poll-options)
- [Search for poll messages »](/api/poll.md#searching-for-polls)
- [Unofficial client warning »](/api/profile.md#unofficial-client-warning)

#### API constants

##### Incoming web events

Incoming web events documented [here &raquo;](/api/web-events.md#event-types).

- Added [`web_app_request_chat` &raquo;](/api/web-events.md#web-app-request-chat)

##### Mini app outgoing events

Outgoing mini app events documented [here &raquo;](/api/bots/webapps.md#incoming-events-client-to-mini-app).

- Added [`requested_chat_failed` &raquo;](/api/bots/webapps.md#requested-chat-failed)
- Added [`requested_chat_sent` &raquo;](/api/bots/webapps.md#requested-chat-sent)

##### Premium features

[Telegram Premium feature identifiers &raquo;](/api/premium.md#telegram-premium-features).

- Added [`ai_compose` &raquo;](/api/premium.md#ai-compose)

##### Premium limits

[Telegram Premium limit identifiers &raquo;](/api/premium.md#double-limits).

- Added [`double_limits__bots_create` &raquo;](/api/premium.md#double-limits-bots-create)

##### Web page type values

Web page type values from the [webPage](/constructor/webPage.md) constructor.

- Added `telegram_newbot`

#### [Schema](/schema.md) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [messages.composeMessageWithAI](/method/messages.composeMessageWithAI.md) - Invokes telegram's AI Editor that can translate, transform, fixup and/or emojify your message in a number of different ways, privately powered by [Cocoon](https://cocoon.org), see [here »](/api/ai.md#compose-messages) for more info!
* Added [messages.reportReadMetrics](/method/messages.reportReadMetrics.md) - Report viewport read metrics for visible messages, indicating how long each message stayed in the chat viewport, see [here »](/api/views.md#read-metrics) for more info on the full flow.
* Added [messages.reportMusicListen](/method/messages.reportMusicListen.md) - Report the listening duration of a music track (audio document without the `voice` flag), see [here »](/api/views.md#music-listens) for more info on the full flow.
* Added [messages.addPollAnswer](/method/messages.addPollAnswer.md) - Add an answer option to an [open-answer poll »](/api/poll.md#open-answer-polls)
* Added [messages.deletePollAnswer](/method/messages.deletePollAnswer.md) - Remove an answer option from an [open-answer poll »](/api/poll.md#open-answer-polls)
* Added [messages.getUnreadPollVotes](/method/messages.getUnreadPollVotes.md) - Get messages containing polls with [unread votes »](/api/poll.md#unread-poll-votes)
* Added [messages.readPollVotes](/method/messages.readPollVotes.md) - Mark all [unread poll votes »](/api/poll.md#unread-poll-votes) in a chat as read
* Added [bots.checkUsername](/method/bots.checkUsername.md) - Check whether a username is available and valid for use when [creating a managed bot »](/api/bots/managed-bots.md#creating-a-managed-bot).
* Added [bots.createBot](/method/bots.createBot.md) - Create a [managed bot »](/api/bots/managed-bots.md#creating-a-managed-bot) owned by the current user and controlled by the specified manager bot.
* Added [bots.exportBotToken](/method/bots.exportBotToken.md) - Export the bot token of a [managed bot »](/api/bots/managed-bots.md#managing-a-managed-bot); can only be called by the manager bot.
* Added [bots.requestWebViewButton](/method/bots.requestWebViewButton.md) - Bots may use this method to prepare a peer request button for a [Mini App](/api/bots/webapps.md), see [here »](/api/bots/buttons.md#requesting-peers-via-mini-apps) for more info.
* Added [bots.getRequestedWebViewButton](/method/bots.getRequestedWebViewButton.md) - Fetch the peer request button a bot prepared for a [Mini App](/api/bots/webapps.md) with [bots.requestWebViewButton](/method/bots.requestWebViewButton.md), invoked when the Mini App emits a [`web_app_request_chat`](/api/web-events.md#web-app-request-chat) event, see [here »](/api/bots/buttons.md#requesting-peers-via-mini-apps) for more info.

##### Changed Methods

* Added **poll_hash** parameter in [messages.getPollResults](/method/messages.getPollResults.md)
* Added **tone** parameter in [messages.translateText](/method/messages.translateText.md)
* Added **flags**, **webapp_req_id** parameters, changed type of **msg_id** from **int** to **flags.0?int** in [messages.sendBotRequestedPeer](/method/messages.sendBotRequestedPeer.md)
* Added **tone** parameter in [messages.summarizeText](/method/messages.summarizeText.md)
* Added **music** parameter in [stories.sendStory](/method/stories.sendStory.md)
* Added **music** parameter in [stories.editStory](/method/stories.editStory.md)

##### New Constructors

* Added [messageActionPollAppendAnswer](/constructor/messageActionPollAppendAnswer.md) - A new answer option was added to an [open-answer poll »](/api/poll.md#open-answer-polls)
* Added [messageActionPollDeleteAnswer](/constructor/messageActionPollDeleteAnswer.md) - An answer option was removed from an [open-answer poll »](/api/poll.md#open-answer-polls)
* Added [messageActionManagedBotCreated](/constructor/messageActionManagedBotCreated.md) - Service message sent by a user to a manager bot when a new [managed bot »](/api/bots/managed-bots.md) is created through it, by invoking [messages.sendBotRequestedPeer](/method/messages.sendBotRequestedPeer.md) with a [requestPeerTypeCreateBot](/constructor/requestPeerTypeCreateBot.md), see [here »](/api/bots/buttons.md#requesting-a-managed-bot) for more info.
* Added [inputMessagesFilterPoll](/constructor/inputMessagesFilterPoll.md) - Filter for poll messages, see [searching for polls »](/api/poll.md#searching-for-polls)
* Added [updateManagedBot](/constructor/updateManagedBot.md) - [Manager bots](/api/bots/managed-bots.md) only: a bot managed by the currently logged in bot was created or updated.
* Added [messageEntityDiffInsert](/constructor/messageEntityDiffInsert.md) - Represents an diff addition: render it by simply <u>underlining</u> the specified section and coloring it in green, see [here »](/api/entities.md#diff-entities) for more info on how to render diff entities.
* Added [messageEntityDiffReplace](/constructor/messageEntityDiffReplace.md) - Represents an diff replacement, render it as follows:
* Added [messageEntityDiffDelete](/constructor/messageEntityDiffDelete.md) - Represents an diff deletion: render it by simply <u>underlining</u> the specified section and coloring it in red, see [here »](/api/entities.md#diff-entities) for more info on how to render diff entities.
* Added [inputPollAnswer](/constructor/inputPollAnswer.md) - An answer option to add to an [open-answer poll »](/api/poll.md#open-answer-polls)
* Added [requestPeerTypeCreateBot](/constructor/requestPeerTypeCreateBot.md) - Used in a [keyboardButtonRequestPeer](/constructor/keyboardButtonRequestPeer.md) by a [manager bot](/api/bots/managed-bots.md) to ask a user to create a new [managed bot »](/api/bots/managed-bots.md), see [here »](/api/bots/buttons.md#requesting-a-managed-bot) for more info.
* Added [inputMessageReadMetric](/constructor/inputMessageReadMetric.md) - Read metric for a single message exposure, describing how long the message was visible in the chat viewport.
* Added [bots.exportedBotToken](/constructor/bots.exportedBotToken.md) - The bot token of a [managed bot »](/api/bots/managed-bots.md#managing-a-managed-bot), exported by the manager bot.
* Added [bots.requestedButton](/constructor/bots.requestedButton.md) - Contains the request ID a bot should pass to a [Mini App](/api/bots/webapps.md) after preparing a peer request button with [bots.requestWebViewButton](/method/bots.requestWebViewButton.md), see [here »](/api/bots/buttons.md#requesting-peers-via-mini-apps) for more info.
* Added [messages.composedMessageWithAI](/constructor/messages.composedMessageWithAI.md) - Represents a message modified by AI.

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **live_photo**, **video** parameters in [inputMediaUploadedPhoto](/constructor/inputMediaUploadedPhoto.md)
* Added **live_photo**, **video** parameters in [inputMediaPhoto](/constructor/inputMediaPhoto.md)
* Added **attached_media**, **solution_media** parameters, changed type of **correct_answers** from **flags.0?Vector&lt;bytes&gt;** to **flags.0?Vector&lt;int&gt;** in [inputMediaPoll](/constructor/inputMediaPoll.md)
* Added **bot_can_manage_bots** parameter in [user](/constructor/user.md)
* Added **live_photo**, **video** parameters in [messageMediaPhoto](/constructor/messageMediaPhoto.md)
* Added **flags**, **attached_media** parameters in [messageMediaPoll](/constructor/messageMediaPoll.md)
* Added **unread_poll_votes_count** parameter in [dialog](/constructor/dialog.md)
* Added **unofficial_security_risk**, **bot_manager_id** parameters in [userFull](/constructor/userFull.md)
* Added **peer**, **msg_id**, **top_msg_id** parameters in [updateMessagePoll](/constructor/updateMessagePoll.md)
* Added **positions** parameter in [updateMessagePollVote](/constructor/updateMessagePollVote.md)
* Added **flags**, **media**, **added_by**, **date** parameters in [pollAnswer](/constructor/pollAnswer.md)
* Added **open_answers**, **revoting_disabled**, **shuffle_answers**, **hide_results_until_close**, **creator**, **hash** parameters in [poll](/constructor/poll.md)
* Added **recent_voters** parameter, changed type of **voters** from **int** to **flags.2?int** in [pollAnswerVoters](/constructor/pollAnswerVoters.md)
* Added **has_unread_votes**, **solution_media** parameters in [pollResults](/constructor/pollResults.md)
* Added **is_app**, **verified_app_name** parameters in [urlAuthResultRequest](/constructor/urlAuthResultRequest.md)
* Added **poll_option** parameter in [messageReplyHeader](/constructor/messageReplyHeader.md)
* Added **unread_poll_votes_count** parameter in [forumTopic](/constructor/forumTopic.md)
* Added **music** parameter in [storyItem](/constructor/storyItem.md)
* Added **poll_option** parameter in [inputReplyToMessage](/constructor/inputReplyToMessage.md)
* Added **poll_votes_notify_from** parameter in [reactionsNotifySettings](/constructor/reactionsNotifySettings.md)

</code></pre></div>

### [Layer 223](/schema?layer=223)

This layer introduces:

- [Login with Telegram via OAuth »](/api/url-authorization.md#oauth-authorization)
- [Content protection for private chats »](/api/content-protection.md#for-users)
- [Group member tags for all users, not just admins »](/api/rank.md)
- [Date styled text entities »](/api/entities.md#date-entities)
- [A new flow to obtain info about the admin that will become the owner of a basic group/supergroup/channel if the original owner leaves »](/api/channel.md#leaving-groups-channels)
- [Ownership transfer for basic groups (along with supergroups and channels) »](/api/channel.md#transferring-ownership-of-a-group-channel)
- [A new filter to return only resale gifts that can be bought using stars](/method/payments.getResaleStarGifts.md)

#### API constants

##### Incoming web events

Incoming web events documented [here &raquo;](/api/web-events.md#event-types).

- Added [`oauth_request` &raquo;](/api/web-events.md#oauth-request)

##### Mini app outgoing events

Outgoing mini app events documented [here &raquo;](/api/bots/webapps.md#incoming-events-client-to-mini-app).

- Added [`oauth_supported` &raquo;](/api/bots/webapps.md#oauth-supported)
- Added [`oauth_result_confirmed` &raquo;](/api/bots/webapps.md#oauth-result-confirmed)
- Added [`oauth_result_failed` &raquo;](/api/bots/webapps.md#oauth-result-failed)

##### Premium features

[Telegram Premium feature identifiers &raquo;](/api/premium.md#telegram-premium-features).

- Added [`pm_noforwards` &raquo;](/api/premium.md#pm-noforwards)

#### [Schema](/schema.md) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [messages.editChatCreator](/method/messages.editChatCreator.md) - Transfer the ownership of a basic group, supergroup or channel to another user, see [here »](/api/channel.md#transferring-ownership-of-a-group-channel) for the full flow.
* Added [messages.getFutureChatCreatorAfterLeave](/method/messages.getFutureChatCreatorAfterLeave.md) - Group/channel owners only: returns the ID of the user that will become the new owner of the group if we decide to leave the group, see [here »](/api/channel.md#leaving-groups-channels) for more info on the full flow.
* Added [messages.editChatParticipantRank](/method/messages.editChatParticipantRank.md) - Edit a group participant's [tag »](/api/rank.md).
* Added [messages.declineUrlAuth](/method/messages.declineUrlAuth.md) - Decline an incoming [OAuth authorization request »](/api/url-authorization.md#oauth-authorization), notifying the server that the user refused the login request.
* Added [messages.checkUrlAuthMatchCode](/method/messages.checkUrlAuthMatchCode.md) - Validate the match code selected by the user against the code shown on the login page, as part of the [OAuth authorization flow »](/api/url-authorization.md#oauth-authorization).

##### Changed Methods

* Added **flags** parameter, changed type of **rank** from **string** to **flags.0?string** in [channels.editAdmin](/method/channels.editAdmin.md)
* Added **in_app_origin** parameter in [messages.requestUrlAuth](/method/messages.requestUrlAuth.md)
* Added **share_phone_number**, **match_code** parameters in [messages.acceptUrlAuth](/method/messages.acceptUrlAuth.md)
* Added **flags**, **request_msg_id** parameters in [messages.toggleNoForwards](/method/messages.toggleNoForwards.md)
* Added **stars_only** parameter in [payments.getResaleStarGifts](/method/payments.getResaleStarGifts.md)

##### Deleted Methods

* Removed channels.editCreator
* Removed channels.getFutureCreatorAfterLeave

##### New Constructors

* Added [messageEntityFormattedDate](/constructor/messageEntityFormattedDate.md) - Represents a specific point in time, rendered as specified [here »](/api/entities.md#date-entities)
* Added [updateChatParticipantRank](/constructor/updateChatParticipantRank.md) - The [tag »](/api/rank.md) of a participant of a [basic group »](/api/channel.md#basic-groups) has changed.
* Added [messageActionNoForwardsToggle](/constructor/messageActionNoForwardsToggle.md) - Emitted only in private chats when enabling or disabling [content protection »](/api/content-protection.md#for-users).
* Added [messageActionNoForwardsRequest](/constructor/messageActionNoForwardsRequest.md) - Emitted only in private chats if the other side requested to disable [content protection »](/api/content-protection.md#for-users).
* Added [channelAdminLogEventActionParticipantEditRank](/constructor/channelAdminLogEventActionParticipantEditRank.md) - A participant's [tag »](/api/rank.md) was changed.

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **flags**, **rank** parameters in [chatParticipant](/constructor/chatParticipant.md)
* Added **from_rank** parameter in [message](/constructor/message.md)
* Added **noforwards_my_enabled**, **noforwards_peer_enabled** parameters in [userFull](/constructor/userFull.md)
* Added **rank** parameter in [channelParticipant](/constructor/channelParticipant.md)
* Added **rank** parameter in [channelParticipantSelf](/constructor/channelParticipantSelf.md)
* Added **flags**, **rank** parameters in [chatParticipantCreator](/constructor/chatParticipantCreator.md)
* Added **flags**, **rank** parameters in [chatParticipantAdmin](/constructor/chatParticipantAdmin.md)
* Added **rank** parameter in [channelParticipantBanned](/constructor/channelParticipantBanned.md)
* Added **edit_rank** parameter in [channelAdminLogEventsFilter](/constructor/channelAdminLogEventsFilter.md)
* Added **manage_ranks** parameter in [chatAdminRights](/constructor/chatAdminRights.md)
* Added **edit_rank** parameter in [chatBannedRights](/constructor/chatBannedRights.md)
* Added **match_codes_first**, **match_codes**, **user_id_hint** parameters in [urlAuthResultRequest](/constructor/urlAuthResultRequest.md)

#### PUSH notification changes

##### New PUSH notifications

* Added [OAUTH_REQUEST](/api/push-updates.md#oauth-request) - `Login request for {1} from {2}`

### [Layer 222](/schema?layer=222)

This layer introduces the following features:

- [Custom keyboard button backgrounds and custom emoji button labels »](/api/bots/buttons.md#button-styles)
- [AI message summarizer »](/api/ai.md#summarize-messages)
- [Gift crafting »](/api/gifts.md#crafting-collectible-gifts)
- [A new flow to obtain info about the admin that will become the owner of a supergroup/channel if the original owner leaves »](/api/channel.md#leaving-groups-channels)

#### API constants

##### Post story content types

Story content types accepted by [post story deep links &raquo;](/api/links.md#post-story-links).

- Added `photo`
- Added `video`
- Added `live`

#### [Schema](/schema.md) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [messages.getEmojiGameInfo](/method/messages.getEmojiGameInfo.md) - Fetch dice game information.
* Added [messages.summarizeText](/method/messages.summarizeText.md) - Summarize the contents of a message with AI, see [here »](/api/ai.md#summarize-messages) for more info.
* Added [payments.getCraftStarGifts](/method/payments.getCraftStarGifts.md) - Obtain owned [collectible gifts »](/api/gifts.md#collectible-gifts) of a specific type that can be used for [crafting »](/api/gifts.md#crafting-collectible-gifts).
* Added [payments.craftStarGift](/method/payments.craftStarGift.md) - Craft a new [collectible gift »](/api/gifts.md#collectible-gifts) by combining 1 to 4 owned collectible gifts of the same base gift type.
* Added [channels.getFutureCreatorAfterLeave](/method/channels.getFutureCreatorAfterLeave.md)

##### Changed Methods

* Added **for_craft** parameter in [payments.getResaleStarGifts](/method/payments.getResaleStarGifts.md)

##### New Constructors

* Added [messages.emojiGameOutcome](/constructor/messages.emojiGameOutcome.md) - Dice game outcome.
* Added [inputMediaStakeDice](/constructor/inputMediaStakeDice.md) - Dice game media.
* Added [messages.emojiGameUnavailable](/constructor/messages.emojiGameUnavailable.md) - Dice game information.
* Added [messages.emojiGameDiceInfo](/constructor/messages.emojiGameDiceInfo.md) - Dice game information.
* Added [updateEmojiGameInfo](/constructor/updateEmojiGameInfo.md) - Dice game update.
* Added [inputPasskeyCredentialFirebasePNV](/constructor/inputPasskeyCredentialFirebasePNV.md) - Alternative [passkey](/api/passkeys.md) credential that proves ownership of the account's phone number through a Firebase Phone Number Verification (PNV) token, used on official apps where a full WebAuthn [public-key passkey](/constructor/inputPasskeyCredentialPublicKey.md) cannot be created, in a way similar to [Firebase SMS authentication »](/api/auth.md).
* Added [starGiftAttributeRarity](/constructor/starGiftAttributeRarity.md) - Exact rarity value for a collectible gift attribute.
* Added [starGiftAttributeRarityUncommon](/constructor/starGiftAttributeRarityUncommon.md) - Represents uncommon collectible gift attribute rarity.
* Added [starGiftAttributeRarityRare](/constructor/starGiftAttributeRarityRare.md) - Represents rare collectible gift attribute rarity.
* Added [starGiftAttributeRarityEpic](/constructor/starGiftAttributeRarityEpic.md) - Represents epic collectible gift attribute rarity.
* Added [starGiftAttributeRarityLegendary](/constructor/starGiftAttributeRarityLegendary.md) - Represents legendary collectible gift attribute rarity.
* Added [updateStarGiftCraftFail](/constructor/updateStarGiftCraftFail.md) - Indicates that a [crafting »](/api/gifts.md#crafting-collectible-gifts) attempt did not produce a new collectible gift.
* Added [keyboardButtonStyle](/constructor/keyboardButtonStyle.md) - This constructor allows setting a custom background color and custom emoji label for a button, see [here »](/api/bots/buttons.md#button-styles) for more info.
* Added [messageActionNewCreatorPending](/constructor/messageActionNewCreatorPending.md) - Service message: emitted to a supergroup when the [group/channel creator leaves the group »](/api/channel.md#leaving-groups-channels), indicating that ownership transfer is pending. The `new_creator_id` user will become the new owner after 7 days if the old owner does not rejoin.
* Added [messageActionChangeCreator](/constructor/messageActionChangeCreator.md) - Service message: emitted to a supergroup when [ownership transfer completes after the old owner left the group »](/api/channel.md#leaving-groups-channels) (7 days after the old owner left without rejoining), indicating that ownership has been transferred to a new owner.

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **summary_from_language** parameter in [message](/constructor/message.md)
* Removed **legacy_ios** parameter in [messageService](/constructor/messageService.md)
* Added **bot_forum_can_manage_topics** parameter in [user](/constructor/user.md)
* Added **flags**, **style** parameters in [keyboardButton](/constructor/keyboardButton.md)
* Added **monoforum** parameter in [channelForbidden](/constructor/channelForbidden.md)
* Added **flags**, **style** parameters in [keyboardButtonUrl](/constructor/keyboardButtonUrl.md)
* Added **style** parameter in [keyboardButtonCallback](/constructor/keyboardButtonCallback.md)
* Added **flags**, **style** parameters in [keyboardButtonRequestPhone](/constructor/keyboardButtonRequestPhone.md)
* Added **flags**, **style** parameters in [keyboardButtonRequestGeoLocation](/constructor/keyboardButtonRequestGeoLocation.md)
* Added **style** parameter in [keyboardButtonSwitchInline](/constructor/keyboardButtonSwitchInline.md)
* Added **flags**, **style** parameters in [keyboardButtonGame](/constructor/keyboardButtonGame.md)
* Added **flags**, **style** parameters in [keyboardButtonBuy](/constructor/keyboardButtonBuy.md)
* Added **style** parameter in [keyboardButtonUrlAuth](/constructor/keyboardButtonUrlAuth.md)
* Added **style** parameter in [inputKeyboardButtonUrlAuth](/constructor/inputKeyboardButtonUrlAuth.md)
* Added **request_phone_number**, **browser**, **platform**, **ip**, **region** parameters in [urlAuthResultRequest](/constructor/urlAuthResultRequest.md)
* Added **flags** parameter, changed type of **url** from **string** to **flags.0?string** in [urlAuthResultAccepted](/constructor/urlAuthResultAccepted.md)
* Added **style** parameter in [keyboardButtonRequestPoll](/constructor/keyboardButtonRequestPoll.md)
* Added **flags**, **game_outcome** parameters in [messageMediaDice](/constructor/messageMediaDice.md)
* Added **flags**, **style** parameters in [inputKeyboardButtonUserProfile](/constructor/inputKeyboardButtonUserProfile.md)
* Added **flags**, **style** parameters in [keyboardButtonUserProfile](/constructor/keyboardButtonUserProfile.md)
* Added **flags**, **style** parameters in [keyboardButtonWebView](/constructor/keyboardButtonWebView.md)
* Added **flags**, **style** parameters in [keyboardButtonSimpleWebView](/constructor/keyboardButtonSimpleWebView.md)
* Added **flags**, **style** parameters in [keyboardButtonRequestPeer](/constructor/keyboardButtonRequestPeer.md)
* Added **style** parameter in [inputKeyboardButtonRequestPeer](/constructor/inputKeyboardButtonRequestPeer.md)
* Added **flags**, **style** parameters in [keyboardButtonCopy](/constructor/keyboardButtonCopy.md)
* Added **flags**, **crafted**, **rarity** parameters, removed **rarity_permille** parameter in [starGiftAttributeModel](/constructor/starGiftAttributeModel.md)
* Added **rarity** parameter, removed **rarity_permille** parameter in [starGiftAttributePattern](/constructor/starGiftAttributePattern.md)
* Added **rarity** parameter, removed **rarity_permille** parameter in [starGiftAttributeBackdrop](/constructor/starGiftAttributeBackdrop.md)
* Added **burned**, **crafted**, **craft_chance_permille** parameters in [starGiftUnique](/constructor/starGiftUnique.md)
* Added **craft**, **can_craft_at** parameters in [messageActionStarGiftUnique](/constructor/messageActionStarGiftUnique.md)
* Added **can_craft_at** parameter in [savedStarGift](/constructor/savedStarGift.md)

#### PUSH notification changes

##### New PUSH notifications

* Added [STORY_LIVE](/api/push-updates.md#story-live) - `started a live stream!`

#### [Changes in the file reference database map file &raquo;](/api/file-references.md)

##### New traversers incoming

* Added <pre><code>traverseMethodResult{name: <a href='/method/payments.getCraftStarGifts'>payments.getCraftStarGifts</a>}</code></pre>

### [Layer 220](/schema?layer=220)

This layer introduces the following features:

- [Added a new suggestion to setup a passkey »](/api/config.md#setup-passkey)
- Added a new premium feature identifier for [paid private messages »](/api/paid-messages.md)
- Some improvements for [auctions »](/api/auctions.md): 
    - Added a detailed description of all auction rounds as [StarGiftAuctionRound](/type/StarGiftAuctionRound.md)s
    - Added `last_gift_num` to [allow computing the user's approximated position »](/api/auctions.md#auction-flow)
    - Added fields to lookup auction gifts currently on resale, see [here »](/api/auctions.md) for more info.
- Added [payments.getStarGiftUpgradeAttributes](/method/payments.getStarGiftUpgradeAttributes.md) to obtain the **full** list of just the collectible attributes that may appear for a gift type once it's upgraded to a [collectible gift »](/api/gifts.md#collectible-gifts).
- Moved some auction-related fields to [starGift](/constructor/starGift.md)
- Added a new [starGiftBackground](/constructor/starGiftBackground.md) constructor to represent the background palette of gift cards and auctions
- [Collectible gift purchase offers »](/api/gifts.md#collectible-gift-purchase-offers)

#### API constants

##### Premium features

[Telegram Premium feature identifiers &raquo;](/api/premium.md#telegram-premium-features).

- Added [`paid_messages` &raquo;](/api/premium.md#paid-messages)

##### Suggested actions

[Basic suggestion identifiers &raquo;](/api/config.md#basic-suggestions)

- Added [`SETUP_PASSKEY` &raquo;](/api/config.md#setup-passkey)

#### [Schema](/schema.md) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [payments.resolveStarGiftOffer](/method/payments.resolveStarGiftOffer.md) - Accept or decline a previously received [collectible gift purchase offer »](/api/gifts.md#collectible-gift-purchase-offers), see [here »](/api/gifts.md#collectible-gift-purchase-offers) for the full flow.
* Added [payments.sendStarGiftOffer](/method/payments.sendStarGiftOffer.md) - Send an offer to purchase a [collectible gift »](/api/gifts.md#collectible-gift-purchase-offers), see [here »](/api/gifts.md#collectible-gift-purchase-offers) for the full flow.
* Added [payments.getStarGiftUpgradeAttributes](/method/payments.getStarGiftUpgradeAttributes.md) - Obtains the **full** list of just the collectible attributes that may appear for a gift type once it's upgraded to a [collectible gift »](/api/gifts.md#collectible-gifts).

##### New Constructors

* Added [messageActionStarGiftPurchaseOffer](/constructor/messageActionStarGiftPurchaseOffer.md) - Contains an offer to purchase a [collectible gift »](/api/gifts.md#collectible-gift-purchase-offers), see [here »](/api/gifts.md#collectible-gift-purchase-offers) for the full flow.
* Added [messageActionStarGiftPurchaseOfferDeclined](/constructor/messageActionStarGiftPurchaseOfferDeclined.md) - A [collectible gift purchase offer »](/api/gifts.md#collectible-gift-purchase-offers) was declined, or the offer expired, see [here »](/api/gifts.md#collectible-gift-purchase-offers) for the full flow.
* Added [starGiftBackground](/constructor/starGiftBackground.md) - Contains the default background palette of a [gift type »](/api/gifts.md#listing-all-possible-collectible-variants).
* Added [starGiftAuctionRound](/constructor/starGiftAuctionRound.md) - Describes one or more [collectible gift auction rounds »](/api/auctions.md).
* Added [starGiftAuctionRoundExtendable](/constructor/starGiftAuctionRoundExtendable.md) - Describes one or more extendable [collectible gift auction rounds »](/api/auctions.md).
* Added [payments.starGiftUpgradeAttributes](/constructor/payments.starGiftUpgradeAttributes.md) - List of just the collectible attributes that may appear for a gift type once it's upgraded to a [collectible gift »](/api/gifts.md#collectible-gifts).

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **legacy_ios** parameter in [messageService](/constructor/messageService.md)
* Added **auction_start_date**, **upgrade_variants**, **background** parameters in [starGift](/constructor/starGift.md)
* Added **gift_num** parameter in [messageActionStarGift](/constructor/messageActionStarGift.md)
* Added **value_usd_amount**, **offer_min_stars** parameters in [starGiftUnique](/constructor/starGiftUnique.md)
* Added **from_offer** parameter in [messageActionStarGiftUnique](/constructor/messageActionStarGiftUnique.md)
* Added **gift_num** parameter in [savedStarGift](/constructor/savedStarGift.md)
* Added **last_gift_num**, **rounds** parameters in [starGiftAuctionState](/constructor/starGiftAuctionState.md)
* Added **flags**, **listed_count**, **fragment_listed_count**, **fragment_listed_url** parameters in [starGiftAuctionStateFinished](/constructor/starGiftAuctionStateFinished.md)
* Added **chats** parameter in [payments.starGiftAuctionState](/constructor/payments.starGiftAuctionState.md)
* Added **gift_num** parameter in [starGiftAuctionAcquiredGift](/constructor/starGiftAuctionAcquiredGift.md)
* Added **chats** parameter in [payments.starGiftActiveAuctions](/constructor/payments.starGiftActiveAuctions.md)
* Removed **center_color**, **edge_color**, **text_color** parameters in [webPageAttributeStarGiftAuction](/constructor/webPageAttributeStarGiftAuction.md)

#### [Changes in the file reference database map file &raquo;](/api/file-references.md)

##### New skipped incoming sources

* Added [payments.starGiftUpgradeAttributes](/constructor/payments.starGiftUpgradeAttributes.md) - Contexts for star gifts are not yet implemented

##### New traversers incoming

* Added <pre><code>traverseIncomingConstructor{predicate: <a href='/constructor/messageActionStarGiftPurchaseOffer'>messageActionStarGiftPurchaseOffer</a>, params: [gift], type: <a href='/type/MessageAction'>MessageAction</a>}</code></pre>
* Added <pre><code>traverseIncomingConstructor{predicate: <a href='/constructor/messageActionStarGiftPurchaseOfferDeclined'>messageActionStarGiftPurchaseOfferDeclined</a>, params: [gift], type: <a href='/type/MessageAction'>MessageAction</a>}</code></pre>
* Added <pre><code>traverseIncomingConstructor{predicate: <a href='/constructor/payments.starGiftUpgradeAttributes'>payments.starGiftUpgradeAttributes</a>, params: [attributes], type: <a href='/type/payments.StarGiftUpgradeAttributes'>payments.StarGiftUpgradeAttributes</a>}</code></pre>
* Added <pre><code>traverseMethodResult{name: <a href='/method/payments.getStarGiftUpgradeAttributes'>payments.getStarGiftUpgradeAttributes</a>}</code></pre>

### [Layer 219](/schema?layer=219)

This layer introduces the following features:

- [Passkeys »](/api/passkeys.md)
- [Message effects »](/api/effects.md) support for [messages.forwardMessages](/method/messages.forwardMessages.md).

#### [Schema](/schema.md) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [account.initPasskeyRegistration](/method/account.initPasskeyRegistration.md) - Initialize passkey registration for the current account, see [here »](/api/passkeys.md#creating-a-passkey) for more info on the full flow.
* Added [account.registerPasskey](/method/account.registerPasskey.md) - Complete passkey registration for the current account, see [here »](/api/passkeys.md#creating-a-passkey) for more info on the full flow.
* Added [account.getPasskeys](/method/account.getPasskeys.md) - List the passkeys associated to the current account that can be used to log in, see [here »](/api/passkeys.md#list-passkeys) for more info on passkeys.
* Added [account.deletePasskey](/method/account.deletePasskey.md) - Delete a passkey associated to the current account, see [here »](/api/passkeys.md#delete-passkeys) for more info.
* Added [auth.initPasskeyLogin](/method/auth.initPasskeyLogin.md) - Initialize login with a passkey over an unauthenticated connection, see [here »](/api/passkeys.md#logging-in-with-a-passkey) for more info.
* Added [auth.finishPasskeyLogin](/method/auth.finishPasskeyLogin.md) - Complete login with a passkey over an unauthenticated connection, see [here »](/api/passkeys.md#logging-in-with-a-passkey) for more info.

##### Changed Methods

* Added **effect** parameter in [messages.forwardMessages](/method/messages.forwardMessages.md)

##### New Constructors

* Added [passkey](/constructor/passkey.md) - Human-readable info about a passkey associated to an account, returned when [creating a passkey »](/api/passkeys.md#creating-a-passkey) or [listing passkeys »](/api/passkeys.md#list-passkeys).
* Added [account.passkeys](/constructor/account.passkeys.md) - List of [passkeys »](/api/passkeys.md#list-passkeys) associated to the current account.
* Added [account.passkeyRegistrationOptions](/constructor/account.passkeyRegistrationOptions.md) - Passkey registration options, see [here »](/api/passkeys.md#creating-a-passkey) for more info on the full flow.
* Added [auth.passkeyLoginOptions](/constructor/auth.passkeyLoginOptions.md) - Passkey login options, see [here »](/api/passkeys.md#logging-in-with-a-passkey) for more info on the full flow.
* Added [inputPasskeyResponseRegister](/constructor/inputPasskeyResponseRegister.md) - WebAuthn registration response used when registering a new passkey, see [here »](/api/passkeys.md#creating-a-passkey) for more info on the full flow.
* Added [inputPasskeyResponseLogin](/constructor/inputPasskeyResponseLogin.md) - WebAuthn authentication response used when logging in with a passkey, see [here »](/api/passkeys.md#logging-in-with-a-passkey) for more info on the full flow.
* Added [inputPasskeyCredentialPublicKey](/constructor/inputPasskeyCredentialPublicKey.md) - Public-key passkey credential used both for registration and for login, see [creating a passkey »](/api/passkeys.md#creating-a-passkey) and [logging in with a passkey »](/api/passkeys.md#logging-in-with-a-passkey) for the full flows.

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **offer** parameter in [starsTransaction](/constructor/starsTransaction.md)

#### [Schema](/schema.md)

### [Layer 218](/schema?layer=218)

This layer introduces the following features:

- [Collectible gift auctions »](/api/auctions.md)

#### API constants

##### Web page type values

Web page type values from the [webPage](/constructor/webPage.md) constructor.

- Added `telegram_auction`

#### [Schema](/schema.md) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [payments.getStarGiftAuctionState](/method/payments.getStarGiftAuctionState.md) - Returns info about a [collectible gift auction »](/api/auctions.md); also subscribes the user to auction updates, see [here »](/api/auctions.md) for more info on the full flow.
* Added [payments.getStarGiftAuctionAcquiredGifts](/method/payments.getStarGiftAuctionAcquiredGifts.md) - Fetches all the gifts that the current user won in an [auction](/api/auctions.md).
* Added [payments.getStarGiftActiveAuctions](/method/payments.getStarGiftActiveAuctions.md) - Fetches all currently active [gift auctions](/api/auctions.md) **the user has ever bid on** (including auctions where the user was outbid and their bid was returned), as long as the auction hasn't ended yet.

##### New Constructors

* Added [inputInvoiceStarGiftAuctionBid](/constructor/inputInvoiceStarGiftAuctionBid.md) - Used to place a bid in a [collectible gift auction »](/api/auctions.md).
* Added [auctionBidLevel](/constructor/auctionBidLevel.md) - Describes a bid in an [auction](/api/auctions.md).
* Added [starGiftAuctionStateNotModified](/constructor/starGiftAuctionStateNotModified.md) - Returned only by auction methods (never by updates) if the passed `version` is equal to the remote [auction »](/api/auctions.md).`version`, meaning auction information hasn't changed over the locally cached version.
* Added [starGiftAuctionState](/constructor/starGiftAuctionState.md) - Represents an active or pending [auction »](/api/auctions.md).
* Added [starGiftAuctionStateFinished](/constructor/starGiftAuctionStateFinished.md) - Represents a finished [auction »](/api/auctions.md).
* Added [starGiftAuctionUserState](/constructor/starGiftAuctionUserState.md) - Contains information about the current user's state in an [auction »](/api/auctions.md).
* Added [updateStarGiftAuctionState](/constructor/updateStarGiftAuctionState.md) - Contains updates to [auction state, see here »](/api/auctions.md) for more info on how to enable these updates.
* Added [updateStarGiftAuctionUserState](/constructor/updateStarGiftAuctionUserState.md) - Contains updates to [auction state related to the current user, see here »](/api/auctions.md) for more info on how to enable these updates.
* Added [payments.starGiftAuctionState](/constructor/payments.starGiftAuctionState.md) - Describes a [collectible gift auction »](/api/auctions.md).
* Added [starGiftAuctionAcquiredGift](/constructor/starGiftAuctionAcquiredGift.md) - Describes a gift that the current user won in an auction.
* Added [payments.starGiftAuctionAcquiredGifts](/constructor/payments.starGiftAuctionAcquiredGifts.md) - Describes all the gifts that the current user won in an [auction](/api/auctions.md).
* Added [starGiftActiveAuctionState](/constructor/starGiftActiveAuctionState.md) - Contains info about [an auction where the user has placed a bid »](/api/auctions.md).
* Added [payments.starGiftActiveAuctionsNotModified](/constructor/payments.starGiftActiveAuctionsNotModified.md) - The list of currently active [gift auctions](/api/auctions.md) **where the user has placed a bid** hasn't changed.
* Added [payments.starGiftActiveAuctions](/constructor/payments.starGiftActiveAuctions.md) - Describes all currently active [gift auctions](/api/auctions.md) **where the user has placed a bid**.
* Added [inputStarGiftAuction](/constructor/inputStarGiftAuction.md) - Used to fetch [auctions](/api/auctions.md) using the ID of the linked [collectible gift](/api/gifts.md#collectible-gifts).
* Added [inputStarGiftAuctionSlug](/constructor/inputStarGiftAuctionSlug.md) - Used to fetch [auctions](/api/auctions.md) using the [slug of an auction deep link »](/api/links.md#auction-links).
* Added [webPageAttributeStarGiftAuction](/constructor/webPageAttributeStarGiftAuction.md) - Contains info about a [collectible gift auction »](/api/auctions.md) for a [webPage](/constructor/webPage.md) preview of an [auction »](/api/auctions.md) (the [webPage](/constructor/webPage.md) will have a `type` of `telegram_auction`).

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **stargift_auction_bid** parameter in [starsTransaction](/constructor/starsTransaction.md)
* Added **auction**, **auction_slug**, **gifts_per_round** parameters in [starGift](/constructor/starGift.md)
* Added **auction_acquired**, **to_id** parameters in [messageActionStarGift](/constructor/messageActionStarGift.md)

#### [Changes in the file reference database map file &raquo;](/api/file-references.md)

##### New traversers incoming

* Added <pre><code>traverseIncomingConstructor{predicate: <a href='/constructor/payments.starGiftActiveAuctions'>payments.starGiftActiveAuctions</a>, params: [auctions], type: <a href='/type/payments.StarGiftActiveAuctions'>payments.StarGiftActiveAuctions</a>}</code></pre>
* Added <pre><code>traverseIncomingConstructor{predicate: <a href='/constructor/payments.starGiftAuctionState'>payments.starGiftAuctionState</a>, params: [gift], type: <a href='/type/payments.StarGiftAuctionState'>payments.StarGiftAuctionState</a>}</code></pre>
* Added <pre><code>traverseIncomingConstructor{predicate: <a href='/constructor/starGiftActiveAuctionState'>starGiftActiveAuctionState</a>, params: [gift], type: <a href='/type/StarGiftActiveAuctionState'>StarGiftActiveAuctionState</a>}</code></pre>
* Added <pre><code>traverseIncomingConstructor{predicate: <a href='/constructor/webPageAttributeStarGiftAuction'>webPageAttributeStarGiftAuction</a>, params: [gift], type: <a href='/type/WebPageAttribute'>WebPageAttribute</a>}</code></pre>
* Added <pre><code>traverseMethodResult{name: <a href='/method/payments.getStarGiftActiveAuctions'>payments.getStarGiftActiveAuctions</a>}</code></pre>
* Added <pre><code>traverseMethodResult{name: <a href='/method/payments.getStarGiftAuctionState'>payments.getStarGiftAuctionState</a>}</code></pre>

### [Layer 217](/schema?layer=217)

This layer introduces:

- [Live stories »](/api/group-calls.md#live-stories)
- [Recent story summaries »](/api/stories.md#recent-story-summaries)
- [Methods to delete group call messages and live story comments »](/api/group-calls.md#in-call-messages)
- [Repeating scheduled messages »](/api/scheduled-messages.md#repeating-scheduled-messages)
- [Privacy settings for the music tab on profiles »](/api/profile.md#music)
- [Anonymous group admins »](/api/rights.md#anonymous-admins) can now [toggle items in checklists »](/api/todo.md#mark-items-as-completed)
- [Suggestions to setup a login email »](/api/auth.md#email-verification)

#### API constants

##### Suggested actions

[Basic suggestion identifiers &raquo;](/api/config.md#basic-suggestions)

- Added [`SETUP_LOGIN_EMAIL` &raquo;](/api/config.md#setup-login-email)
- Added [`SETUP_LOGIN_EMAIL_NOSKIP` &raquo;](/api/config.md#setup-login-email-noskip)

#### [Schema](/schema.md) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [stories.startLive](/method/stories.startLive.md) - Start a live story, optionally using RTMP livestream mode, see [here »](/api/group-calls.md#live-stories) for the full flow.
* Added [phone.deleteGroupCallMessages](/method/phone.deleteGroupCallMessages.md) - Delete specific messages from the [in-call message overlay »](/api/group-calls.md#in-call-messages) of a video chat/livestream or live story, including in RTMP mode.
* Added [phone.deleteGroupCallParticipantMessages](/method/phone.deleteGroupCallParticipantMessages.md) - As an admin, delete all messages from a specific participant in the [in-call message overlay »](/api/group-calls.md#in-call-messages) of a video chat/livestream or live story, including in RTMP mode.
* Added [phone.getGroupCallStars](/method/phone.getGroupCallStars.md) - Fetch a live story's total donations and top donors, see [paid live story donations »](/api/group-calls.md#paid-live-story-donations).
* Added [phone.saveDefaultSendAs](/method/phone.saveDefaultSendAs.md) - Save the default peer displayed as the author of live story comments and reactions, see [in-call messages »](/api/group-calls.md#in-call-messages).

##### Changed Methods

* Added **schedule_repeat_period** parameter in [messages.sendMessage](/method/messages.sendMessage.md)
* Added **schedule_repeat_period** parameter in [messages.sendMedia](/method/messages.sendMedia.md)
* Added **schedule_repeat_period** parameter in [messages.forwardMessages](/method/messages.forwardMessages.md)
* Added **schedule_repeat_period** parameter in [messages.editMessage](/method/messages.editMessage.md)
* Added **send_paid_messages_stars** parameter in [phone.toggleGroupCallSettings](/method/phone.toggleGroupCallSettings.md)
* Added **for_live_stories** parameter in [channels.getSendAs](/method/channels.getSendAs.md)
* Added **flags**, **live_story** parameters in [phone.getGroupCallStreamRtmpUrl](/method/phone.getGroupCallStreamRtmpUrl.md)
* Changed type of [stories.getPeerMaxIDs](/method/stories.getPeerMaxIDs.md) from **Vector&lt;int&gt;** to **Vector&lt;RecentStory&gt;**
* Changed type of [phone.sendGroupCallMessage](/method/phone.sendGroupCallMessage.md) from **Bool** to **Updates**
* Added **flags**, **allow_paid_stars**, **send_as** parameters in [phone.sendGroupCallMessage](/method/phone.sendGroupCallMessage.md)

##### New Constructors

* Added [messageMediaVideoStream](/constructor/messageMediaVideoStream.md) - Identifies the active group call associated with a [live story »](/api/group-calls.md#live-stories).
* Added [updateDeleteGroupCallMessages](/constructor/updateDeleteGroupCallMessages.md) - Indicates that messages were deleted from the [in-call message overlay »](/api/group-calls.md#in-call-messages) of a video chat/livestream or live story, including in RTMP mode.
* Added [groupCallMessage](/constructor/groupCallMessage.md) - Represents an [in-call message »](/api/group-calls.md#in-call-messages), emoji reaction, paid live story comment or standalone paid live story donation.
* Added [inputPrivacyKeySavedMusic](/constructor/inputPrivacyKeySavedMusic.md) - Whether the user can see the [songs we pinned to our profile »](/api/profile.md#music).
* Added [privacyKeySavedMusic](/constructor/privacyKeySavedMusic.md) - Whether the user can see the [songs we pinned to our profile »](/api/profile.md#music).
* Added [groupCallDonor](/constructor/groupCallDonor.md) - Describes a live story donor in the [donation leaderboard »](/api/group-calls.md#paid-live-story-donations).
* Added [phone.groupCallStars](/constructor/phone.groupCallStars.md) - Contains a live story's total donations and top donors, see [paid live story donations »](/api/group-calls.md#paid-live-story-donations).
* Added [recentStory](/constructor/recentStory.md) - Summary of a peer's [active stories »](/api/stories.md#recent-story-summaries), embedded in [user](/constructor/user.md).`stories_max_id` and [channel](/constructor/channel.md).`stories_max_id` and returned by [stories.getPeerMaxIDs](/method/stories.getPeerMaxIDs.md).

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **schedule_repeat_period** parameter in [message](/constructor/message.md)
* Changed type of **stories_max_id** from **flags2.5?int** to **flags2.5?RecentStory** in [user](/constructor/user.md)
* Changed type of **stories_max_id** from **flags2.4?int** to **flags2.4?RecentStory** in [channel](/constructor/channel.md)
* Added **send_paid_messages_stars**, **default_send_as** parameters in [groupCall](/constructor/groupCall.md)
* Added **paid_stars_total** parameter in [groupCallParticipant](/constructor/groupCallParticipant.md)
* Added **live_story**, **peer** parameters, removed **chat_id** parameter in [updateGroupCall](/constructor/updateGroupCall.md)
* Added **days** parameter, removed **months** parameter in [messageActionGiftPremium](/constructor/messageActionGiftPremium.md)
* Added **live** parameter in [storyItemSkipped](/constructor/storyItemSkipped.md)
* Added **days** parameter, removed **months** parameter in [payments.checkedGiftCode](/constructor/payments.checkedGiftCode.md)
* Added **days** parameter, removed **months** parameter in [messageActionGiftCode](/constructor/messageActionGiftCode.md)
* Added **phonegroup_message** parameter in [starsTransaction](/constructor/starsTransaction.md)
* Added **disallow_stargifts_from_channels** parameter in [disallowedGiftsSettings](/constructor/disallowedGiftsSettings.md)
* Changed type of **completed_by** from **long** to **Peer** in [todoCompletion](/constructor/todoCompletion.md)
* Removed **from_id**, **random_id** parameters, changed type of **message** from **TextWithEntities** to **GroupCallMessage** in [updateGroupCallMessage](/constructor/updateGroupCallMessage.md)

### [Layer 216](/schema?layer=216)

This layer introduces the following new features:

- [Bot forums »](/api/forum.md#bot-forums)
- [Private contact notes »](/api/contacts.md#private-notes-for-contacts)
- [Suggested birthdays »](/api/profile.md#birthday)
- [Collectible gifts can now have associated message color palettes »](/api/colors.md#collectible-message-palettes)
- [Live bot response streaming »](/api/bots/ai.md#live-response-streaming).
- [Hosted collectible gifts: linking a collectible gift on the TON blockchain to a Telegram profile »](/api/gifts.md#hosted-collectible-gifts)

#### API constants

##### Client configs

[Client configuration keys &raquo;](/api/config.md#client-configuration).

- Added [`contact_note_length_limit` &raquo;](/api/config.md#contact-note-length-limit)
- Added [`group_call_message_length_limit` &raquo;](/api/config.md#group-call-message-length-limit)
- Added [`group_call_message_ttl` &raquo;](/api/config.md#group-call-message-ttl)
- Added [`message_typing_draft_ttl` &raquo;](/api/config.md#message-typing-draft-ttl)

#### [Schema](/schema.md) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [messages.getForumTopics](/method/messages.getForumTopics.md) - Get [topics of a forum](/api/forum.md)
* Added [messages.getForumTopicsByID](/method/messages.getForumTopicsByID.md) - Get forum topics by their ID
* Added [messages.editForumTopic](/method/messages.editForumTopic.md) - Edit [forum topic](/api/forum.md).
* Added [messages.updatePinnedForumTopic](/method/messages.updatePinnedForumTopic.md) - Pin or unpin [forum topics](/api/forum.md)
* Added [messages.reorderPinnedForumTopics](/method/messages.reorderPinnedForumTopics.md) - Reorder pinned forum topics
* Added [messages.createForumTopic](/method/messages.createForumTopic.md) - Create a [forum topic](/api/forum.md).
* Added [messages.deleteTopicHistory](/method/messages.deleteTopicHistory.md) - Delete message history of a [forum topic](/api/forum.md)
* Added [phone.sendGroupCallMessage](/method/phone.sendGroupCallMessage.md) - Send an in-call message to all participants of a video chat/livestream or live story, including in RTMP mode, see [here »](/api/group-calls.md#in-call-messages) for more info.
* Added [phone.sendGroupCallEncryptedMessage](/method/phone.sendGroupCallEncryptedMessage.md) - Send an E2E-encrypted message or emoji reaction to all participants of a conference call. This method can only be used with conferences; see [here »](/api/end-to-end/group-calls.md#conference-in-call-messages) for the serialization and encryption process.
* Added [contacts.updateContactNote](/method/contacts.updateContactNote.md) - Update the private note associated to a contact; see [here »](/api/contacts.md#private-notes-for-contacts) for more info.
* Added [users.suggestBirthday](/method/users.suggestBirthday.md) - Suggest a birthday to another user, see [here »](/api/profile.md#birthday) for more info on birthdays in the API.

##### Changed Methods

* Added **note** parameter in [contacts.addContact](/method/contacts.addContact.md)
* Added **messages_enabled** parameter in [phone.toggleGroupCallSettings](/method/phone.toggleGroupCallSettings.md)
* Removed **background_emoji_id** parameter, changed type of **color** from **flags.2?int** to **flags.2?PeerColor** in [account.updateColor](/method/account.updateColor.md)
* Added **peer_color_available**, **exclude_hosted** parameters in [payments.getSavedStarGifts](/method/payments.getSavedStarGifts.md)

##### Deleted Methods

* Removed channels.createForumTopic
* Removed channels.getForumTopics
* Removed channels.getForumTopicsByID
* Removed channels.editForumTopic
* Removed channels.updatePinnedForumTopic
* Removed channels.deleteTopicHistory
* Removed channels.reorderPinnedForumTopics

##### New Constructors

* Added [sendMessageTextDraftAction](/constructor/sendMessageTextDraftAction.md) - Used by bots to implement [live message streaming »](/api/bots/ai.md#live-response-streaming).
* Added [updateGroupCallMessage](/constructor/updateGroupCallMessage.md) - A new message, reaction, paid comment or donation was received through the [in-call message overlay »](/api/group-calls.md#in-call-messages).
* Added [updateGroupCallEncryptedMessage](/constructor/updateGroupCallEncryptedMessage.md) - A new E2E-encrypted message or emoji reaction was received in a conference call, see [here »](/api/end-to-end/group-calls.md#receiving-and-decrypting-a-message) for the decryption process.
* Added [updatePinnedForumTopic](/constructor/updatePinnedForumTopic.md) - A [forum topic »](/api/forum.md#forum-topics) was pinned or unpinned.
* Added [updatePinnedForumTopics](/constructor/updatePinnedForumTopics.md) - The [pinned topics](/api/forum.md#forum-topics) of a forum have changed.
* Added [peerColorCollectible](/constructor/peerColorCollectible.md) - Represents a [color palette »](/api/colors.md) associated to a [collectible gift »](/api/gifts.md#collectible-gifts), see [here »](/api/colors.md#collectible-gift-palettes) for more info.
* Added [inputPeerColorCollectible](/constructor/inputPeerColorCollectible.md) - Represents a [color palette »](/api/colors.md) associated to a [collectible gift »](/api/gifts.md#collectible-gifts), see [here »](/api/colors.md#collectible-gift-palettes) for more info.
* Added [messageActionSuggestBirthday](/constructor/messageActionSuggestBirthday.md) - A new birthday was suggested using [users.suggestBirthday](/method/users.suggestBirthday.md), see [here »](/api/profile.md#birthday) for more info on birthdays in the API.

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **flags**, **note** parameters in [inputPhoneContact](/constructor/inputPhoneContact.md)
* Added **note** parameter in [userFull](/constructor/userFull.md)
* Added **topics** parameter in [messages.messages](/constructor/messages.messages.md)
* Added **topics** parameter in [messages.messagesSlice](/constructor/messages.messagesSlice.md)
* Added **flags**, **top_msg_id** parameters in [updateUserTyping](/constructor/updateUserTyping.md)
* Added **top_msg_id** parameter in [updateReadHistoryInbox](/constructor/updateReadHistoryInbox.md)
* Added **bot_forum_view** parameter in [user](/constructor/user.md)
* Added **messages_enabled**, **can_change_messages_enabled**, **min** parameters in [groupCall](/constructor/groupCall.md)
* Added **title_missing**, **peer** parameters in [forumTopic](/constructor/forumTopic.md)
* Added **title_missing** parameter in [messageActionTopicCreate](/constructor/messageActionTopicCreate.md)
* Added **peer_color_available** parameter in [starGift](/constructor/starGift.md)
* Added **peer_color**, **host_id** parameters in [starGiftUnique](/constructor/starGiftUnique.md)
* Added **assigned** parameter in [messageActionStarGiftUnique](/constructor/messageActionStarGiftUnique.md)

##### Deleted Constructors

* Removed updateChannelPinnedTopic
* Removed updateChannelPinnedTopics

#### PUSH notification changes

##### New PUSH notifications

* Added [MESSAGE_SUGGEST_BIRTHDAY](/api/push-updates.md#message-suggest-birthday) - `{1} suggested you your birthday`

#### [Changes in the file reference database map file &raquo;](/api/file-references.md)

##### New traversers incoming

* Added <pre><code>traverseMethodResult{name: <a href='/method/messages.getForumTopics'>messages.getForumTopics</a>}</code></pre>
* Added <pre><code>traverseMethodResult{name: <a href='/method/messages.getForumTopicsByID'>messages.getForumTopicsByID</a>}</code></pre>

##### Deleted Traversers incoming

* Removed <pre><code>traverseMethodResult{name: <a href='/method/channels.getForumTopics'>channels.getForumTopics</a>}</code></pre>
* Removed <pre><code>traverseMethodResult{name: <a href='/method/channels.getForumTopicsByID'>channels.getForumTopicsByID</a>}</code></pre>

#### [End-to-end schema](/schema/end-to-end.md) changes

##### New Constructors

* Added [jsonObjectValue](/constructor/jsonObjectValue.md) - JSON key: value pair
* Added [jsonNull](/constructor/jsonNull.md) - null JSON value
* Added [jsonBool](/constructor/jsonBool.md) - JSON boolean value
* Added [jsonNumber](/constructor/jsonNumber.md) - JSON numeric value
* Added [jsonString](/constructor/jsonString.md) - JSON string
* Added [jsonArray](/constructor/jsonArray.md) - JSON array
* Added [jsonObject](/constructor/jsonObject.md) - JSON object value
* Added [textWithEntities](/constructor/textWithEntities.md) - Styled text with [message entities](/api/entities.md)
* Added [groupCallMessage](/constructor/groupCallMessage.md) - Represents an [in-call message »](/api/group-calls.md#in-call-messages), emoji reaction, paid live story comment or standalone paid live story donation.

### [Layer 215](/schema?layer=215)

This layer introduces the following new features and improvements:

- [Allow removing the starGiftAttributeOriginalDetails from an owned gift »](/api/gifts.md#dropping-the-original-details-of-an-upgraded-gift).
- [payments.starGiftUpgradePreview](/constructor/payments.starGiftUpgradePreview.md) now returns a list of future [gift upgrade](/api/gifts.md#collectible-gifts) prices in **prices** and **next\_prices**.
- Improved pagination for [account.getUniqueGiftChatThemes](/method/account.getUniqueGiftChatThemes.md), also added a note indicating that the method may return less than `limit` results, even if the actual number of remaining results is `>= limit`, pagination should be used to fetch them all.

#### [Schema](/schema.md) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [auth.checkPaidAuth](/method/auth.checkPaidAuth.md) - Checks the status of a [login payment](/api/auth.md#paid-auth).

##### Changed Methods

* Changed type of **offset** from **int** to **string** in [account.getUniqueGiftChatThemes](/method/account.getUniqueGiftChatThemes.md)

##### New Constructors

* Added [inputInvoicePremiumAuthCode](/constructor/inputInvoicePremiumAuthCode.md) - Used to pay for login codes, in case of high cost of SMS verification codes for the user's country/provider, see [here »](/api/auth.md#paid-auth) for more info.
* Added [inputInvoiceStarGiftDropOriginalDetails](/constructor/inputInvoiceStarGiftDropOriginalDetails.md) - Used to pay for for the removal of the [starGiftAttributeOriginalDetails](/constructor/starGiftAttributeOriginalDetails.md) attribute from a collectible gift, see [here »](/api/gifts.md#dropping-the-original-details-of-an-upgraded-gift) for the full flow.
* Added [starGiftUpgradePrice](/constructor/starGiftUpgradePrice.md) - Indicates the price for a [gift upgrade »](/api/gifts.md#collectible-gifts) starting from a specific point in time.

##### Changed Constructors

* Changed type of **next_offset** from **flags.0?int** to **flags.0?string** in [account.chatThemes](/constructor/account.chatThemes.md)
* Added **stargift_drop_original_details** parameter in [starsTransaction](/constructor/starsTransaction.md)
* Added **drop_original_details_stars** parameter in [messageActionStarGiftUnique](/constructor/messageActionStarGiftUnique.md)
* Added **prices**, **next_prices** parameters in [payments.starGiftUpgradePreview](/constructor/payments.starGiftUpgradePreview.md)
* Added **drop_original_details_stars** parameter in [savedStarGift](/constructor/savedStarGift.md)
* Added **currency**, **amount** parameters in [auth.sentCodePaymentRequired](/constructor/auth.sentCodePaymentRequired.md)
