### [Layer 214](/schema?layer=214)

To view all changes since the last documentation refresh, start reading at [layer 196](#layer-196). This documentation refresh *also* brings the following changes:

- [A brand new file reference database map file »](/api/file-references.md): can be used to automatically codegen a fully functional file reference database, see [here »](/api/file-references.md) for more info.
    
    
    - The map file generator is open source, and is specifically designed to be easy to run on newer, even experimental layers, see [here for more info »](/api/file-reference-generator.md).
    - Layer diffs will now list changes to the file reference map file as well.
- [The RPC error database was updated »](/api/errors.md), and it now has the following extra fields:
    
    
    - `business_supported` - Contains the full list of methods that can be used by bots over a [business connection with invokeWithBusinessConnection](/api/bots/connected-business-bots.md).
    - `unauthed_allowed` - Contains the full list of methods that can be used by not yet logged in connections.
- All methods in the documentation now explicitly state if they can be used by bots, users or both, and if they can be used over a business connection or over an unauthorized connection.
- Updated the list of methods that can be used over a [business connection](/api/bots/connected-business-bots.md)
    
    
    - [stories.sendStory](/method/stories.sendStory.md) and [stories.editStory](/method/stories.editStory.md) can also be used to post and edit stories on behalf of a connected business account: in this case, simply pass the business account's peer in `peer`, *without* wrapping the request in an [invokeWithBusinessConnection »](/method/invokeWithBusinessConnection.md) query.  
        Note that [stories.editStory](/method/stories.editStory.md) can only be used to edit stories posted by the same business bot on behalf of the user.
- Updated the list of methods that can be used over an [unauthed connection](/api/auth.md#we-are-authorized)
- The [documentation for the profile page was also reorganized, to include the full list of profile tabs to show, along with instructions on how to populate them »](/api/profile.md).
- [Corrected hash generation instructions for messages.getScheduledHistory, the correct order is id, edit\_date, date; not id, date, edit\_date »](/method/messages.getScheduledHistory.md)
- Added documentation on [frozen accounts »](/api/auth.md#frozen-accounts)
- [Added a new client configuration key for the maximum number of allowed quiz poll answers](/api/config.md#poll-answers-max)
- [Added a new client configuration key for the maximum amount of Telegram Stars that can be withdrawn from a channel or bot's balance »](/api/config.md#stars-revenue-withdrawal-max)
- [Added support for age verification, if required by the current country's legislation »](/api/age-verification.md).
- Added device storage and secure storage APIs for [mini apps](/api/bots/webapps.md), using the following events:
    
    
    - [web\_app\_device\_storage\_save\_key](/api/web-events.md#web-app-device-storage-save-key) - Save or remove a value from the device's local storage associated with this user and mini app
    - [web\_app\_device\_storage\_get\_key](/api/web-events.md#web-app-device-storage-get-key) - Get a value from the device's local storage associated with this user and mini app
    - [web\_app\_device\_storage\_clear](/api/web-events.md#web-app-device-storage-clear) - Clear the device's local storage associated with this user and mini app
    - [web\_app\_secure\_storage\_save\_key](/api/web-events.md#web-app-secure-storage-save-key) - Save or remove a value from the device's secure storage associated with this user and mini app
    - [web\_app\_secure\_storage\_get\_key](/api/web-events.md#web-app-secure-storage-get-key) - Get a value from the device's secure storage associated with this user and mini app
    - [web\_app\_secure\_storage\_restore\_key](/api/web-events.md#web-app-secure-storage-restore-key) - Restore a value to the device's secure storage associated with this user and mini app
    - [web\_app\_secure\_storage\_clear](/api/web-events.md#web-app-secure-storage-clear) - Clear the device's secure storage associated with this user and mini app
- Add support for the new `manage_direct_messages` [admin right](/constructor/chatAdminRights.md) in [group/channel bot links](/api/links.md#group-channel-bot-links).
- Updated the documentation for bot API dialog IDs to include support for the new monoforum ID range, see [here »](/api/bots/ids.md#monoforum-ids) for more info.
    
    
    - Specifically, the bot API dialog ID new ranges from `-4000000000000` to `1099511627775` (previously the range was `-2002147483648` to `1099511627775`).
    - The transformed range for bot API monoforum dialog IDs is `-2002147483649` to `-4000000000000` inclusively.
- Push notifications can now have a custom `report_delivery_until_date` parameter for [Telegram Gateway verification messages](https://telegram.org/blog/star-messages-gateway-2-0-and-more#save-even-more-on-user-verification): if set, the message is a Telegram Gateway verification code, and [messages.reportMessagesDelivery](/method/messages.reportMessagesDelivery.md) should be invoked with the `push` flag set.
- Added a correction to the [paid reactions documentation »](/api/reactions.md#paid-reactions): the `random_id` argument of [messages.sendPaidReaction](/method/messages.sendPaidReaction.md) **must** be composed of a 64-bit integer where the lower 32 bits are random, and the higher 32 bits **are equal to the current unixtime**, i.e. `uint64_t random_id = (time() << 32) | ((uint64_t)random_uint32_t())`: this differs from the `random_id` format of all other methods in the API, which just take 64 random bits.
- Clarified that adding a new recent reaction should trigger modification of the cached list and regeneration of the hash with a custom algorithm, see [here »](/api/reactions.md#recent-reactions) for more info.
- Clarified that modifying saved message tags should trigger modification of the cached list and regeneration of the hash with a custom algorithm, see [here »](/api/saved-messages.md#tags) for more info.
- The [webpage type list »](/constructor/webPage.md) was updated.
- [Clarified](/api/invoking.md) that in some cases such as updates from big channels, the API may return constructors from older layers, different from the connection's current layer.  
    Clients should treat this as a `500` server error, and handle it by closing and reopening the TCP socket, re-reinitializing the session with [initConnection](/method/initConnection.md) and invoking [getDifference](/api/updates.md).
- A whole bunch of new features and articles, [start reading at layer 196](#layer-196) to view them all. And here are the changes in this layer:
- [Set a collectible gift as chat theme »](/api/themes.md#chat-themes)
- Allow sending an email to Telegram's support team when getting [auth.sentCodePaymentRequired](/constructor/auth.sentCodePaymentRequired.md) during login (official clients only).

#### [Schema](/schema.md) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [account.getUniqueGiftChatThemes](/method/account.getUniqueGiftChatThemes.md) - Obtain all [chat themes »](/api/themes.md#chat-themes) associated to owned or [hosted collectible gifts »](/api/gifts.md#hosted-collectible-gifts).

##### Changed Methods

* Added **theme** parameter, removed **emoticon** parameter in [messages.setChatTheme](/method/messages.setChatTheme.md)

##### New Constructors

* Added [chatTheme](/constructor/chatTheme.md) - A chat theme
* Added [chatThemeUniqueGift](/constructor/chatThemeUniqueGift.md) - A chat theme based on a [collectible gift »](/api/gifts.md#collectible-gifts).
* Added [account.chatThemesNotModified](/constructor/account.chatThemesNotModified.md) - The available chat themes were not modified
* Added [account.chatThemes](/constructor/account.chatThemes.md) - Available [chat themes](/api/themes.md#chat-themes)
* Added [inputChatThemeEmpty](/constructor/inputChatThemeEmpty.md) - Remove any currently configured theme.
* Added [inputChatTheme](/constructor/inputChatTheme.md) - Set an emoji-based chat theme, returned by [account.getChatThemes](/method/account.getChatThemes.md).
* Added [inputChatThemeUniqueGift](/constructor/inputChatThemeUniqueGift.md) - Set a theme based on an owned or [hosted collectible gift »](/api/gifts.md#hosted-collectible-gifts), returned by [account.getUniqueGiftChatThemes](/method/account.getUniqueGiftChatThemes.md).

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **theme** parameter, removed **emoticon** parameter in [messageActionSetChatTheme](/constructor/messageActionSetChatTheme.md)
* Added **support_email_address**, **support_email_subject** parameters in [auth.sentCodePaymentRequired](/constructor/auth.sentCodePaymentRequired.md)
* Added **theme** parameter, removed **theme_emoticon** parameter in [userFull](/constructor/userFull.md)
* Added **theme_available**, **theme_peer** parameters in [starGiftUnique](/constructor/starGiftUnique.md)

#### PUSH notification changes

##### New PUSH notifications

* Added [MESSAGE_STARGIFT_UNPACK_UPGRADE](/api/push-updates.md#message-stargift-unpack-upgrade) - `{1} unpacked the Gift that you helped upgrade`

##### New skipped incoming sources

* Added [messages.getSponsoredMessages](/method/messages.getSponsoredMessages.md) - Do not store file references from sponsored messages
* Added [help.getAppUpdate](/method/help.getAppUpdate.md) - Don't handle file references from ephemeral app update info
* Added [help.getRecentMeUrls](/method/help.getRecentMeUrls.md) - Don't handle file references from recent t.me URLs
* Added [recentMeUrlChatInvite](/constructor/recentMeUrlChatInvite.md) - Do not store references based on chat invite links
* Added [messages.checkChatInvite](/method/messages.checkChatInvite.md) - Do not store references based on chat invite links
* Added [messages.getInlineBotResults](/method/messages.getInlineBotResults.md) - Inline bot results are ephemeral
* Added [messages.getPreparedInlineMessage](/method/messages.getPreparedInlineMessage.md) - Inline bot results are ephemeral
* Added [messages.uploadMedia](/method/messages.uploadMedia.md) - A freshly uploaded media file will obtain a context only once it is sent to a chat
* Added [messages.uploadImportedMedia](/method/messages.uploadImportedMedia.md) - A freshly uploaded media file will obtain a context only once it is sent to a chat
* Added [updateServiceNotification](/constructor/updateServiceNotification.md) - Cannot refetch service notifications
* Added [messages.getWebPagePreview](/method/messages.getWebPagePreview.md) - No locations are added for the method call, as it doesn't use persistent IDs as input; the location is instead extracted from the persistent IDs in the returned WebPage object
* Added [payments.resaleStarGifts](/constructor/payments.resaleStarGifts.md) - Contexts for star gifts are not yet implemented
* Added [payments.starGiftUpgradePreview](/constructor/payments.starGiftUpgradePreview.md) - Contexts for star gifts are not yet implemented
* Added [starGift](/constructor/starGift.md) - Contexts for star gifts are not yet implemented
* Added [starGiftUnique](/constructor/starGiftUnique.md) - Contexts for star gifts are not yet implemented
* Added [starGiftCollection](/constructor/starGiftCollection.md) - Contexts for star gifts are not yet implemented
* Added [payments.starGiftCollections](/constructor/payments.starGiftCollections.md) - Contexts for star gifts are not yet implemented
* Added [messages.getCustomEmojiDocuments](/method/messages.getCustomEmojiDocuments.md) - Do not store file references in this context
* Added [account.uploadTheme](/method/account.uploadTheme.md) - A freshly uploaded theme file will obtain a context only once it is created via account.createTheme

##### New refresh actions

* Added fileSourceMessage - <pre><code>[getMessageOp](/api/file-references.md#getmessageop)(peer: getInputPeerByIdOp(peer), id: copyOp(id), quick_reply_shortcut_id: copyOp(quick_reply_shortcut_id))</code></pre>
* Added fileSourceScheduledMessage - <pre><code><a href='/method/messages.getScheduledMessages'>messages.getScheduledMessages</a>(peer: getInputPeerByIdOp(peer), id: [copyOp(id)])</code></pre>
* Added fileSourceStory - <pre><code><a href='/method/stories.getStoriesByID'>stories.getStoriesByID</a>(id: [copyOp(id)], peer: getInputPeerByIdOp(peer))</code></pre>
* Added fileSourceWebPage - <pre><code><a href='/method/messages.getWebPage'>messages.getWebPage</a>(url: copyOp(url), hash: 0)</code></pre>
* Added fileSourceBotApp - <pre><code><a href='/method/messages.getBotApp'>messages.getBotApp</a>(app: <a href='/constructor/inputBotAppID'>inputBotAppID</a>{id: copyOp(id), access_hash: copyOp(access_hash)}, hash: 0)</code></pre>
* Added fileSourceUserFull - <pre><code><a href='/method/users.getFullUser'>users.getFullUser</a>(id: getInputUserByIdOp(id))</code></pre>
* Added fileSourceAdminLog - <pre><code><a href='/method/channels.getAdminLog'>channels.getAdminLog</a>(channel: getInputChannelByIdOp(channel), max_id: copyOp(max_id), min_id: copyOp(max_id), limit: 1, q: "")</code></pre>
* Added fileSourceStoryAlbum - <pre><code><a href='/method/stories.getAlbums'>stories.getAlbums</a>(peer: getInputPeerByIdOp(peer), hash: 0)</code></pre>
* Added fileSourceBotPreviewMedia - <pre><code><a href='/method/bots.getPreviewMedias'>bots.getPreviewMedias</a>(bot: getInputUserByIdOp(bot))</code></pre>
* Added fileSourceBotPreviewInfo - <pre><code><a href='/method/bots.getPreviewInfo'>bots.getPreviewInfo</a>(bot: getInputUserByIdOp(bot), lang_code: copyOp(lang_code))</code></pre>
* Added fileSourcePaidMedia - <pre><code><a href='/method/messages.getExtendedMedia'>messages.getExtendedMedia</a>(id: [copyOp(id)], peer: getInputPeerByIdOp(peer))</code></pre>
* Added fileSourceSavedMusic - <pre><code><a href='/method/users.getSavedMusicByID'>users.getSavedMusicByID</a>(id: getInputUserByIdOp(user_id), documents: [<a href='/constructor/inputDocument'>inputDocument</a>{id: copyOp(id), access_hash: copyOp(access_hash), file_reference: base64_decode("")}])</code></pre>
* Added fileSourceChatFull - <pre><code><a href='/method/messages.getFullChat'>messages.getFullChat</a>(chat_id: copyOp(chat_id))</code></pre>

### [Layer 213](/schema?layer=213)

This layer introduces the following new features:

- [Changing the default profile tab »](/api/profile.md#tabs)
- [Saving music to the profile »](/api/profile.md#music)
- [Added a `locked_until_date` flag to starGift, for locked gifts that cannot be sent until the specified date](/constructor/starGift.md).
- [Added the payments.checkCanSendGift method, to check if a non-locked gift can't be sent yet for other reasons](/method/payments.checkCanSendGift.md).
- [Added an optional `spend_purpose_peer` flag to inputStorePaymentStarsTopup, that should be populated with the peer where the topup process was initiated due to low funds (i.e. a bot for bot payments, a channel for paid media/reactions, etc) »](/constructor/inputStorePaymentStarsTopup.md)[Documentation for the profile page was also reorganized, to include the full list of tabs to show, along with instructions on how to populate them »](/api/profile.md).

#### [Schema](/schema.md) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [account.setMainProfileTab](/method/account.setMainProfileTab.md) - Changes the main profile tab of the current user, see [here »](/api/profile.md#tabs) for more info.
* Added [account.saveMusic](/method/account.saveMusic.md) - Adds or removes a song from the current user's profile [see here »](/api/profile.md#music) for more info on the music tab of the profile page.
* Added [account.getSavedMusicIds](/method/account.getSavedMusicIds.md) - Fetch the full list of only the IDs of [songs currently added to the profile, see here »](/api/profile.md#music) for more info.
* Added [users.getSavedMusic](/method/users.getSavedMusic.md) - Get songs [pinned to the user's profile, see here »](/api/profile.md#music) for more info.
* Added [users.getSavedMusicByID](/method/users.getSavedMusicByID.md) - Check if the passed songs are still pinned to the user's profile, or refresh the file references of songs pinned on a user's profile [see here »](/api/profile.md#music) for more info.
* Added [channels.setMainProfileTab](/method/channels.setMainProfileTab.md) - Changes the main profile tab of a channel, see [here »](/api/profile.md#tabs) for more info.
* Added [payments.checkCanSendGift](/method/payments.checkCanSendGift.md) - Check if the specified [gift »](/api/gifts.md) can be sent.

##### New Constructors

* Added [profileTabPosts](/constructor/profileTabPosts.md) - Represents the [stories](/api/stories.md) tab of a profile page.
* Added [profileTabGifts](/constructor/profileTabGifts.md) - Represents the [gifts](/api/gifts.md) tab of a profile page.
* Added [profileTabMedia](/constructor/profileTabMedia.md) - Represents the media tab of a profile page.
* Added [profileTabFiles](/constructor/profileTabFiles.md) - Represents the shared files tab of a profile.
* Added [profileTabMusic](/constructor/profileTabMusic.md) - Represents the [music](/api/profile.md#music) tab of a profile page.
* Added [profileTabVoice](/constructor/profileTabVoice.md) - Represents the voice messages tab of a profile page.
* Added [profileTabLinks](/constructor/profileTabLinks.md) - Represents the shared links tab of a profile page.
* Added [profileTabGifs](/constructor/profileTabGifs.md) - Represents the gifs tab of a profile page.
* Added [users.savedMusicNotModified](/constructor/users.savedMusicNotModified.md) - This subset of the songs currently pinned on a user's profile hasn't changed, see [here »](/api/profile.md#music) for more info.
* Added [users.savedMusic](/constructor/users.savedMusic.md) - List of songs currently pinned on a user's profile, see [here »](/api/profile.md#music) for more info.
* Added [account.savedMusicIdsNotModified](/constructor/account.savedMusicIdsNotModified.md) - The list of IDs of songs ([document](/constructor/document.md).`id`s) currently pinned on our profile hasn't changed.
* Added [account.savedMusicIds](/constructor/account.savedMusicIds.md) - List of IDs of songs ([document](/constructor/document.md).`id`s) currently pinned on our profile, see [here »](/api/profile.md#music) for more info.
* Added [payments.checkCanSendGiftResultOk](/constructor/payments.checkCanSendGiftResultOk.md) - The specified gift can be sent.
* Added [payments.checkCanSendGiftResultFail](/constructor/payments.checkCanSendGiftResultFail.md) - The specified gift cannot be sent yet for the specified reason.

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **main_tab** parameter in [channelFull](/constructor/channelFull.md)
* Added **upgrade_separate** parameter in [messageActionStarGift](/constructor/messageActionStarGift.md)
* Added **main_tab**, **saved_music** parameters in [userFull](/constructor/userFull.md)
* Added **flags**, **spend_purpose_peer** parameters in [inputStorePaymentStarsTopup](/constructor/inputStorePaymentStarsTopup.md)
* Added **locked_until_date** parameter in [starGift](/constructor/starGift.md)
* Added **chats** parameter in [payments.uniqueStarGift](/constructor/payments.uniqueStarGift.md)
* Added **chats** parameter in [messages.webPagePreview](/constructor/messages.webPagePreview.md)
* Added **upgrade_separate** parameter in [savedStarGift](/constructor/savedStarGift.md)

### [Layer 212](/schema?layer=212)

This layer introduces the following features:

- [Separately prepay for the upgrade of a gift »](/api/gifts.md#prepaying-for-someone-elses-upgrade).
- [Get info about the value of a collectible gift »](/method/payments.getUniqueStarGiftValueInfo.md)

#### [Schema](/schema.md) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [payments.getUniqueStarGiftValueInfo](/method/payments.getUniqueStarGiftValueInfo.md) - Get information about the value of a [collectible gift »](/api/gifts.md#collectible-gifts).

##### Changed Methods

* Added **exclude_upgradable**, **exclude_unupgradable** parameters, removed **exclude_limited** parameter in [payments.getSavedStarGifts](/method/payments.getSavedStarGifts.md)

##### New Constructors

* Added [inputInvoiceStarGiftPrepaidUpgrade](/constructor/inputInvoiceStarGiftPrepaidUpgrade.md) - [Separately prepay for the upgrade of a gift »](/api/gifts.md#prepaying-for-someone-elses-upgrade).
* Added [payments.uniqueStarGiftValueInfo](/constructor/payments.uniqueStarGiftValueInfo.md) - Information about the value of a [collectible gift »](/api/gifts.md#collectible-gifts).

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **prepaid_upgrade**, **prepaid_upgrade_hash**, **gift_msg_id** parameters in [messageActionStarGift](/constructor/messageActionStarGift.md)
* Added **prepaid_upgrade** parameter in [messageActionStarGiftUnique](/constructor/messageActionStarGiftUnique.md)
* Added **posts_search**, **stargift_prepaid_upgrade** parameters in [starsTransaction](/constructor/starsTransaction.md)
* Added **gift_id**, **value_amount**, **value_currency** parameters in [starGiftUnique](/constructor/starGiftUnique.md)
* Added **prepaid_upgrade_hash** parameter in [savedStarGift](/constructor/savedStarGift.md)

#### [End-to-end group call schema &raquo;](/api/end-to-end/group-calls.md) changes

##### New Constructors

* Added [e2e.chain.groupBroadcastNonceCommit](/constructor/e2e.chain.groupBroadcastNonceCommit.md) - Commit message for the [E2E conference call emoji verification protocol](/api/end-to-end/group-calls.md#commit-reveal-protocol-workflow).
* Added [e2e.chain.groupBroadcastNonceReveal](/constructor/e2e.chain.groupBroadcastNonceReveal.md) - Reveal message for the [E2E conference call emoji verification protocol](/api/end-to-end/group-calls.md#commit-reveal-protocol-workflow).
* Added [e2e.chain.groupParticipant](/constructor/e2e.chain.groupParticipant.md) - A participant and their permissions in an [E2E conference call group state »](/api/end-to-end/group-calls.md#participants-and-permissions).
* Added [e2e.chain.groupState](/constructor/e2e.chain.groupState.md) - The participant list and external permission mask for an [E2E conference call blockchain state »](/api/end-to-end/group-calls.md#participants-and-permissions).
* Added [e2e.chain.sharedKey](/constructor/e2e.chain.sharedKey.md) - Encrypted [shared group key material](/api/end-to-end/group-calls.md#shared-key-encryption) for an E2E conference call.
* Added [e2e.chain.changeNoop](/constructor/e2e.chain.changeNoop.md) - A no-operation [E2E conference call blockchain change](/api/end-to-end/group-calls.md#change-types-for-group-calls), potentially used only to randomize the resulting block hash.
* Added [e2e.chain.changeSetValue](/constructor/e2e.chain.changeSetValue.md) - Updates the [key-value trie state for an E2E conference call blockchain](/api/end-to-end/group-calls.md#change-types-for-group-calls).
* Added [e2e.chain.changeSetGroupState](/constructor/e2e.chain.changeSetGroupState.md) - Updates the [E2E conference call participant list and permissions](/api/end-to-end/group-calls.md#change-types-for-group-calls), clearing the current shared key state.
* Added [e2e.chain.changeSetSharedKey](/constructor/e2e.chain.changeSetSharedKey.md) - Establishes a new shared encryption key for an E2E conference call, see [change types for group calls »](/api/end-to-end/group-calls.md#change-types-for-group-calls).
* Added [e2e.chain.stateProof](/constructor/e2e.chain.stateProof.md) - Proof of the [E2E conference call blockchain state](/api/end-to-end/group-calls.md#block-application-process) after a block has been applied.
* Added [e2e.chain.block](/constructor/e2e.chain.block.md) - A block in the [main E2E conference call blockchain](/api/end-to-end/group-calls.md#block-structure), containing state changes for the call and the resulting state proof.
* Added [e2e.callPacket](/constructor/e2e.callPacket.md) - Marker constructor whose CRC32 constructor ID is used when deriving and authenticating [encrypted group call packets](/api/end-to-end/group-calls.md#packet-encryption).
* Added [e2e.callPacketLargeMsgId](/constructor/e2e.callPacketLargeMsgId.md) - Marker constructor whose CRC32 constructor ID is used when signing the large message ID produced while encrypting [group call packets](/api/end-to-end/group-calls.md#packet-encryption).

### [Layer 211](/schema?layer=211)

This layer introduces:

- [Story albums »](/api/stories.md#story-albums)
- [Star gift collection link previews »](/constructor/webPageAttributeStarGiftCollection.md)
- [Full text global channel post search »](/api/search.md#posts-tab)
- [Allow specification of the resale price of a collectible gift in TON »](/constructor/messageActionStarGiftUnique.md)
- [Pending star ratings »](/api/stars.md#star-rating)

#### [Schema](/schema.md) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [channels.checkSearchPostsFlood](/method/channels.checkSearchPostsFlood.md) - Check if the specified [global post search »](/api/search.md#posts-tab) requires payment.
* Added [stories.createAlbum](/method/stories.createAlbum.md) - Creates a [story album](/api/stories.md#story-albums).
* Added [stories.updateAlbum](/method/stories.updateAlbum.md) - Rename a [story albums »](/api/stories.md#story-albums), or add, delete or reorder stories in it.
* Added [stories.reorderAlbums](/method/stories.reorderAlbums.md) - Reorder [story albums on a profile »](/api/stories.md#story-albums).
* Added [stories.deleteAlbum](/method/stories.deleteAlbum.md) - Delete a [story album](/api/stories.md#story-albums).
* Added [stories.getAlbums](/method/stories.getAlbums.md) - Get [story albums](/api/stories.md#story-albums) created by a peer.
* Added [stories.getAlbumStories](/method/stories.getAlbumStories.md) - Get stories in a [story album »](/api/stories.md#story-albums).

##### Changed Methods

* Added **flags**, **query**, **allow_paid_stars** parameters, changed type of **hashtag** from **string** to **flags.0?string** in [channels.searchPosts](/method/channels.searchPosts.md)
* Added **resell_amount** parameter, removed **resell_stars** parameter in [payments.updateStarGiftPrice](/method/payments.updateStarGiftPrice.md)
* Added **albums** parameter in [stories.sendStory](/method/stories.sendStory.md)

##### New Constructors

* Added [webPageAttributeStarGiftCollection](/constructor/webPageAttributeStarGiftCollection.md) - Contains info about a [gift collection »](/api/gifts.md#gift-collections) for a [webPage](/constructor/webPage.md) preview of a [gift collection »](/api/gifts.md#gift-collections) (the [webPage](/constructor/webPage.md) will have a `type` of `telegram_collection`).
* Added [storyAlbum](/constructor/storyAlbum.md) - Represents a [story album »](/api/stories.md#story-albums).
* Added [stories.albumsNotModified](/constructor/stories.albumsNotModified.md) - The [story album list »](/api/stories.md#story-albums) hasn't changed.
* Added [stories.albums](/constructor/stories.albums.md) - [Story albums »](/api/stories.md#story-albums).
* Added [searchPostsFlood](/constructor/searchPostsFlood.md) - Indicates if the specified [global post search »](/api/search.md#posts-tab) requires payment.

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **resale_amount** parameter, removed **resale_stars** parameter in [messageActionStarGiftUnique](/constructor/messageActionStarGiftUnique.md)
* Added **stars_my_pending_rating**, **stars_my_pending_rating_date** parameters in [userFull](/constructor/userFull.md)
* Added **search_flood** parameter in [messages.messagesSlice](/constructor/messages.messagesSlice.md)
* Added **flags**, **ton** parameters in [inputInvoiceStarGiftResale](/constructor/inputInvoiceStarGiftResale.md)
* Added **albums** parameter in [storyItem](/constructor/storyItem.md)
* Added **resale_ton_only**, **resell_amount** parameters, removed **resell_stars** parameter in [starGiftUnique](/constructor/starGiftUnique.md)

### [Layer 210](/schema?layer=210)

This layer introduces:

- [Star gift collections »](/api/gifts.md#gift-collections)
- Premium-only gifts (`require_premium`) and per-user gift limits for certain gift types (`per_user_total`, `per_user_remains`).
- [Star ratings »](/api/stars.md#star-rating)

#### [Schema](/schema.md) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [payments.createStarGiftCollection](/method/payments.createStarGiftCollection.md) - Create a [star gift collection »](/api/gifts.md#gift-collections).
* Added [payments.updateStarGiftCollection](/method/payments.updateStarGiftCollection.md) - Add or remove gifts from a [star gift collection »](/api/gifts.md#gift-collections), or rename the collection.
* Added [payments.reorderStarGiftCollections](/method/payments.reorderStarGiftCollections.md) - Reorder the [star gift collections »](/api/gifts.md#gift-collections) on an owned peer's profile.
* Added [payments.deleteStarGiftCollection](/method/payments.deleteStarGiftCollection.md) - Delete a [star gift collection »](/api/gifts.md#gift-collections).
* Added [payments.getStarGiftCollections](/method/payments.getStarGiftCollections.md) - Fetches all [star gift collections »](/api/gifts.md#gift-collections) of a peer.

##### Changed Methods

* Added **collection_id** parameter in [payments.getSavedStarGifts](/method/payments.getSavedStarGifts.md)

##### New Constructors

* Added [starsRating](/constructor/starsRating.md) - Represents the profile's [star rating, see here »](/api/stars.md#star-rating) for more info.
* Added [starGiftCollection](/constructor/starGiftCollection.md) - Represents a [star gift collection »](/api/gifts.md#gift-collections).
* Added [payments.starGiftCollectionsNotModified](/constructor/payments.starGiftCollectionsNotModified.md) - The list of [star gift collections »](/api/gifts.md#gift-collections) hasn't changed.
* Added [payments.starGiftCollections](/constructor/payments.starGiftCollections.md) - Represents a list of [star gift collections »](/api/gifts.md#gift-collections).

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **stars_rating** parameter in [userFull](/constructor/userFull.md)
* Added **require_premium**, **limited_per_user**, **per_user_total**, **per_user_remains** parameters in [starGift](/constructor/starGift.md)
* Added **require_premium** parameter in [starGiftUnique](/constructor/starGiftUnique.md)
* Added **collection_id** parameter in [savedStarGift](/constructor/savedStarGift.md)

### [Layer 208](/schema?layer=208)

This layer introduces:

- [Suggested channel posts »](/api/suggested-posts.md)
- Add a new `manage_direct_messages` [admin right](/constructor/chatAdminRights.md)
- Add support for the new `manage_direct_messages` [admin right](/constructor/chatAdminRights.md) in [group/channel bot links](/api/links.md#group-channel-bot-links)
- [Allow replying to specific todo tasks »](/api/todo.md)
- Unify channel revenue statistics and withdrawal methods with the equivalent ones used for stars, by simply adding a `ton` flag and the missing parameters: 
    - [Balance and transaction history »](/api/stars.md#balance-and-transaction-history)
    - [Revenue statistics »](/api/stars.md#revenue-statistics)
    - [Withdrawing revenue »](/api/stars.md#withdrawing-revenue)
- In some places in the API, it's possible to use Grams instead of Stars, see [here »](/api/stars.md#gram) for more info.

#### [Schema](/schema.md) changes

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **todo_item_id** parameter in [messageReplyHeader](/constructor/messageReplyHeader.md)
* Added **todo_item_id** parameter in [inputReplyToMessage](/constructor/inputReplyToMessage.md)
* Removed **posts_search** parameter in [starsTransaction](/constructor/starsTransaction.md)

### [Layer 207](/schema?layer=207)

This layer introduces:

- [Suggested posts »](/api/suggested-posts.md), letting users propose paid posts to channels, with approval, success and refund [service messages](/constructor/messageActionSuggestedPostApproval.md)
- A new `manage_direct_messages` [admin right](/constructor/chatAdminRights.md)
- The ability to gift, hold and withdraw [Gram »](/api/stars.md#gram) alongside Telegram Stars: TON balances and transactions in the stars status/transaction/revenue methods, TON gifts ([messageActionGiftTon](/constructor/messageActionGiftTon.md), [starsTonAmount](/constructor/starsTonAmount.md)), and the unification of channel revenue statistics and withdrawals with the equivalent Stars methods
- [Global post search »](/api/search.md#posts-tab)

#### [Schema](/schema.md) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [messages.toggleSuggestedPostApproval](/method/messages.toggleSuggestedPostApproval.md) - Approve or reject a [suggested post »](/api/suggested-posts.md).

##### Changed Methods

* Added **suggested_post** parameter in [messages.sendMessage](/method/messages.sendMessage.md)
* Added **suggested_post** parameter in [messages.sendMedia](/method/messages.sendMedia.md)
* Added **suggested_post** parameter in [messages.forwardMessages](/method/messages.forwardMessages.md)
* Added **suggested_post** parameter in [messages.saveDraft](/method/messages.saveDraft.md)
* Added **flags**, **ton** parameters in [payments.getStarsStatus](/method/payments.getStarsStatus.md)
* Added **ton** parameter in [payments.getStarsTransactions](/method/payments.getStarsTransactions.md)
* Added **ton** parameter in [payments.getStarsRevenueStats](/method/payments.getStarsRevenueStats.md)
* Added **flags**, **ton**, **amount** parameters, removed **stars** parameter in [payments.getStarsRevenueWithdrawalUrl](/method/payments.getStarsRevenueWithdrawalUrl.md)
* Added **flags**, **ton** parameters in [payments.getStarsTransactionsByID](/method/payments.getStarsTransactionsByID.md)

##### Deleted Methods

* Removed stats.getBroadcastRevenueStats
* Removed stats.getBroadcastRevenueWithdrawalUrl
* Removed stats.getBroadcastRevenueTransactions

##### New Constructors

* Added [suggestedPost](/constructor/suggestedPost.md) - Contains info about a [suggested post »](/api/suggested-posts.md).
* Added [messageActionSuggestedPostApproval](/constructor/messageActionSuggestedPostApproval.md) - A [suggested post »](/api/suggested-posts.md) was approved or rejected.
* Added [messageActionSuggestedPostSuccess](/constructor/messageActionSuggestedPostSuccess.md) - A [suggested post »](/api/suggested-posts.md) was successfully posted, and payment for it was successfully received.
* Added [messageActionSuggestedPostRefund](/constructor/messageActionSuggestedPostRefund.md) - A [suggested post »](/api/suggested-posts.md) was accepted and posted or scheduled, but either the channel deleted the posted/scheduled post before [stars\_suggested\_post\_age\_min](/api/config.md#stars-suggested-post-age-min) seconds have elapsed, or the user refunded the payment for the stars used to pay for the suggested post.
* Added [starsTonAmount](/constructor/starsTonAmount.md) - Describes an amount of Gram in nanograms (i.e. `1/1_000_000_000` of a Gram).
* Added [messageActionGiftTon](/constructor/messageActionGiftTon.md) - You were gifted some Grams.
* Added [inputStickerSetTonGifts](/constructor/inputStickerSetTonGifts.md) - TON gifts stickerset.

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **paid_suggested_post_stars**, **paid_suggested_post_ton**, **suggested_post** parameters in [message](/constructor/message.md)
* Added **suggested_post** parameter in [draftMessage](/constructor/draftMessage.md)
* Added **manage_direct_messages** parameter in [chatAdminRights](/constructor/chatAdminRights.md)
* Added **posts_search**, **amount**, **ads_proceeds_from_date**, **ads_proceeds_to_date** parameters, removed **stars** parameter in [starsTransaction](/constructor/starsTransaction.md)
* Added **flags**, **top_hours_graph** parameters in [payments.starsRevenueStats](/constructor/payments.starsRevenueStats.md)
* Added **released_by** parameter in [starGift](/constructor/starGift.md)
* Added **chats**, **users** parameters in [payments.starGifts](/constructor/payments.starGifts.md)
* Added **released_by** parameter in [starGiftUnique](/constructor/starGiftUnique.md)

##### Deleted Constructors

* Removed stats.broadcastRevenueStats
* Removed stats.broadcastRevenueWithdrawalUrl
* Removed broadcastRevenueTransactionProceeds
* Removed broadcastRevenueTransactionWithdrawal
* Removed broadcastRevenueTransactionRefund
* Removed stats.broadcastRevenueTransactions
* Removed broadcastRevenueBalances
* Removed updateBroadcastRevenueTransactions

### [Layer 205](/schema?layer=205)

This layer introduces:

- [Sponsored messages to show on channel videos »](/api/sponsored-messages.md#getting-sponsored-video-advertisements)
- [To-do lists »](/api/todo.md)
- [Rename account.addNoPaidMessagesException to account.toggleNoPaidMessagesException, and add a `require_payment` flag to refund all Stars transferred to us by a peer with paid messages »](/method/account.toggleNoPaidMessagesException.md)
- Allow invoking [account.toggleNoPaidMessagesException](/method/account.toggleNoPaidMessagesException.md) for monoforum topics, and add a new [updateMonoForumNoPaidException](/constructor/updateMonoForumNoPaidException.md) to signal changes to [monoForumDialog](/constructor/monoForumDialog.md).`nopaid_messages_exception` to other admins and to other currently logged in sessions of the current monoforum admin.

#### [Schema](/schema.md) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [messages.toggleTodoCompleted](/method/messages.toggleTodoCompleted.md) - Mark one or more items of a [todo list »](/api/todo.md) as completed or not completed.
* Added [messages.appendTodoList](/method/messages.appendTodoList.md) - Appends one or more items to a [todo list »](/api/todo.md).
* Added [account.toggleNoPaidMessagesException](/method/account.toggleNoPaidMessagesException.md) - Allow a user to send us messages without paying if [paid messages »](/api/paid-messages.md) are enabled.

##### Changed Methods

* Added **flags**, **msg_id** parameters in [messages.getSponsoredMessages](/method/messages.getSponsoredMessages.md)
* Added **flags**, **parent_peer** parameters in [account.getPaidMessagesRevenue](/method/account.getPaidMessagesRevenue.md)

##### Deleted Methods

* Removed account.addNoPaidMessagesException

##### New Constructors

* Added [todoItem](/constructor/todoItem.md) - An item of a [todo list »](/api/todo.md).
* Added [todoList](/constructor/todoList.md) - Represents a [todo list »](/api/todo.md).
* Added [todoCompletion](/constructor/todoCompletion.md) - A completed [todo list »](/api/todo.md) item.
* Added [inputMediaTodo](/constructor/inputMediaTodo.md) - Creates a [todo list »](/api/todo.md).
* Added [messageMediaToDo](/constructor/messageMediaToDo.md) - Represents a [todo list »](/api/todo.md).
* Added [messageActionTodoCompletions](/constructor/messageActionTodoCompletions.md) - Items were marked as completed or not completed in a [todo list »](/api/todo.md).
* Added [messageActionTodoAppendTasks](/constructor/messageActionTodoAppendTasks.md) - Items were appended to the [todo list »](/api/todo.md).
* Added [updateMonoForumNoPaidException](/constructor/updateMonoForumNoPaidException.md) - An admin has (un)exempted this [monoforum topic »](/api/monoforum.md) from payment to send messages using [account.toggleNoPaidMessagesException](/method/account.toggleNoPaidMessagesException.md).

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **saved_peer_id** parameter in [messageService](/constructor/messageService.md)
* Added **send_paid_messages_stars** parameter in [channelFull](/constructor/channelFull.md)
* Added **min_display_duration**, **max_display_duration** parameters in [sponsoredMessage](/constructor/sponsoredMessage.md)
* Added **start_delay**, **between_delay** parameters in [messages.sponsoredMessages](/constructor/messages.sponsoredMessages.md)
* Added **nopaid_messages_exception** parameter in [monoForumDialog](/constructor/monoForumDialog.md)

#### PUSH notification changes

##### New PUSH notifications

* Added [CHANNEL_MESSAGE_TODO](/api/push-updates.md#channel-message-todo) - `{1} posted a checklist {2}`
* Added [CHANNEL_MESSAGE_TODO_APPEND](/api/push-updates.md#channel-message-todo-append) - `{1} added {2} tasks`
* Added [CHANNEL_MESSAGE_TODO_DONE](/api/push-updates.md#channel-message-todo-done) - `{1} toggled {2} tasks`
* Added [CHAT_MESSAGE_TODO](/api/push-updates.md#chat-message-todo) - `{1} sent a checklist {3} to the group {2}`
* Added [CHAT_MESSAGE_TODO_APPEND](/api/push-updates.md#chat-message-todo-append) - `{1} added {3} tasks in the group {2}`
* Added [CHAT_MESSAGE_TODO_DONE](/api/push-updates.md#chat-message-todo-done) - `{1} toggled {3} tasks in the group {2}`
* Added [CHAT_REACT_TODO](/api/push-updates.md#chat-react-todo) - `{1}: {3} to your checklist {4} in {2}`
* Added [MESSAGE_TODO](/api/push-updates.md#message-todo) - `{1} sent you a checklist {2}`
* Added [PINNED_TODO](/api/push-updates.md#pinned-todo) - `{1} pinned a checklist {2}`
* Added [REACT_TODO](/api/push-updates.md#react-todo) - `{1}: {2} to your checklist {3}`

#### [Schema](/schema.md)

### [Layer 204](/schema?layer=204)

This layer introduces:

- [Monoforums »](/api/monoforum.md)
- [Bot API monoforum IDs »](/api/bots/ids.md#monoforum-ids)
- [Tabbed forum UI »](/api/forum.md#tabbed-or-list-based-forum-ui)
- All flags added to [channel](/constructor/channel.md) (listed below) are valid `min` flags, meaning they must be applied over the locally stored version even if the `min` flag of the incoming [channel](/constructor/channel.md) is set.

#### [Schema](/schema.md) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [messages.getSavedDialogsByID](/method/messages.getSavedDialogsByID.md) - Obtain information about specific [saved message dialogs »](/api/saved-messages.md#saved-message-dialogs) or [monoforum topics »](/api/monoforum.md).
* Added [messages.readSavedHistory](/method/messages.readSavedHistory.md) - Mark messages as read in a [monoforum topic »](/api/monoforum.md).
* Added [channels.getMessageAuthor](/method/channels.getMessageAuthor.md) - Can only be invoked by non-bot admins of a [monoforum »](/api/monoforum.md), obtains the original sender of a message sent by other monoforum admins to the monoforum, on behalf of the channel associated to the monoforum.

##### Changed Methods

* Added **reply_to** parameter in [messages.forwardMessages](/method/messages.forwardMessages.md)
* Added **parent_peer** parameter in [messages.markDialogUnread](/method/messages.markDialogUnread.md)
* Added **flags**, **parent_peer** parameters in [messages.getDialogUnreadMarks](/method/messages.getDialogUnreadMarks.md)
* Added **saved_peer_id** parameter in [messages.unpinAllMessages](/method/messages.unpinAllMessages.md)
* Added **saved_peer_id** parameter in [messages.getUnreadReactions](/method/messages.getUnreadReactions.md)
* Added **saved_peer_id** parameter in [messages.readReactions](/method/messages.readReactions.md)
* Added **tabs** parameter in [channels.toggleForum](/method/channels.toggleForum.md)
* Added **parent_peer** parameter in [messages.getSavedDialogs](/method/messages.getSavedDialogs.md)
* Added **flags**, **parent_peer** parameters in [messages.getSavedHistory](/method/messages.getSavedHistory.md)
* Added **parent_peer** parameter in [messages.deleteSavedHistory](/method/messages.deleteSavedHistory.md)
* Added **flags**, **broadcast_messages_allowed** parameters in [channels.updatePaidMessagesPrice](/method/channels.updatePaidMessagesPrice.md)

##### New Constructors

* Added [inputReplyToMonoForum](/constructor/inputReplyToMonoForum.md) - Used to send messages to a [monoforum topic](/api/monoforum.md).
* Added [monoForumDialog](/constructor/monoForumDialog.md) - Represents a [monoforum topic »](/api/monoforum.md).
* Added [updateReadMonoForumInbox](/constructor/updateReadMonoForumInbox.md) - Incoming messages in a [monoforum topic](/api/monoforum.md) were read
* Added [updateReadMonoForumOutbox](/constructor/updateReadMonoForumOutbox.md) - Outgoing messages in a [monoforum](/api/monoforum.md) were read.

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **broadcast_messages_allowed**, **monoforum**, **forum_tabs**, **linked_monoforum_id** parameters in [channel](/constructor/channel.md)
* Added **saved_peer_id** parameter in [updateDraftMessage](/constructor/updateDraftMessage.md)
* Added **saved_peer_id** parameter in [updateChannelReadMessagesContents](/constructor/updateChannelReadMessagesContents.md)
* Added **saved_peer_id** parameter in [updateDialogUnreadMark](/constructor/updateDialogUnreadMark.md)
* Added **saved_peer_id** parameter in [updateMessageReactions](/constructor/updateMessageReactions.md)
* Added **monoforum_peer_id** parameter in [inputReplyToMessage](/constructor/inputReplyToMessage.md)
* Added **flags**, **broadcast_messages_allowed** parameters in [messageActionPaidMessagesPrice](/constructor/messageActionPaidMessagesPrice.md)

### [Layer 203](/schema?layer=203)

This layer introduces:

- [Methods to resell collectible gifts »](/api/gifts.md#reselling-collectible-gifts)
- [Autotranslation for all users of a channel »](/api/translation.md#autotranslation-for-channels)
- [stories.canSendStory now returns the number of available active story slots »](/method/stories.canSendStory.md)
- [Collectible gift links »](/api/links.md#collectible-gift-link)
- [Custom suggestions »](/api/config.md#custom-suggestions)
- [All suggestion fields were moved from the client configuration object to help.getPromoData »](/api/config.md#suggestions)
- All flags added to [channel](/constructor/channel.md) (listed below) are valid `min` flags, meaning they must be applied over the locally stored version even if the `min` flag of the incoming [channel](/constructor/channel.md) is set.

#### [Schema](/schema.md) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [payments.getResaleStarGifts](/method/payments.getResaleStarGifts.md) - Get [collectible gifts](/api/gifts.md#collectible-gifts) of a specific type currently on resale, see [here »](/api/gifts.md#reselling-collectible-gifts) for more info.
* Added [payments.updateStarGiftPrice](/method/payments.updateStarGiftPrice.md) - A [collectible gift we own »](/api/gifts.md#collectible-gifts) can be put up for sale on the [gift marketplace »](https://telegram.org/blog/gift-marketplace-and-more) with this method, see [here »](/api/gifts.md#reselling-collectible-gifts) for more info.
* Added [channels.toggleAutotranslation](/method/channels.toggleAutotranslation.md) - Toggle autotranslation in a channel, for all users: see [here »](/api/translation.md#autotranslation-for-channels) for more info.

##### Changed Methods

* Changed type of [stories.canSendStory](/method/stories.canSendStory.md) from **Bool** to **stories.CanSendStoryCount**

##### New Constructors

* Added [inputSavedStarGiftSlug](/constructor/inputSavedStarGiftSlug.md) - Points to a [collectible gift obtained from a collectible gift link »](/api/links.md#collectible-gift-link).
* Added [starGiftAttributeIdModel](/constructor/starGiftAttributeIdModel.md) - The ID of a model of a [collectible gift »](/api/gifts.md#collectible-gifts).
* Added [starGiftAttributeIdPattern](/constructor/starGiftAttributeIdPattern.md) - The ID of a pattern of a [collectible gift »](/api/gifts.md#collectible-gifts).
* Added [starGiftAttributeIdBackdrop](/constructor/starGiftAttributeIdBackdrop.md) - The ID of a backdrop of a [collectible gift »](/api/gifts.md#collectible-gifts).
* Added [starGiftAttributeCounter](/constructor/starGiftAttributeCounter.md) - Indicates the total number of gifts that have the specified attribute.
* Added [payments.resaleStarGifts](/constructor/payments.resaleStarGifts.md) - List of gifts currently on [resale »](/api/gifts.md#reselling-collectible-gifts).
* Added [inputInvoiceStarGiftResale](/constructor/inputInvoiceStarGiftResale.md) - Used to buy a [collectible gift](/api/gifts.md#collectible-gifts) currently up on resale, see [here](/api/gifts.md#reselling-collectible-gifts) for more info on the full flow.
* Added [channelAdminLogEventActionToggleAutotranslation](/constructor/channelAdminLogEventActionToggleAutotranslation.md) - [Channel autotranslation was toggled »](/api/translation.md#autotranslation-for-channels).
* Added [stories.canSendStoryCount](/constructor/stories.canSendStoryCount.md) - Contains the number of available active story slots (equal to the value of the [`story_expiring_limit_*` client configuration parameter](/api/config.md#story-expiring-limit-default) minus the number of currently active stories).
* Added [pendingSuggestion](/constructor/pendingSuggestion.md) - Represents a [custom pending suggestion »](/api/config.md#custom-suggestions).

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **autotranslation** parameter in [channel](/constructor/channel.md)
* Added **pending_suggestions**, **dismissed_suggestions**, **custom_pending_suggestion** parameters, changed type of **peer** from **Peer** to **flags.3?Peer** in [help.promoData](/constructor/help.promoData.md)
* Added **availability_resale**, **resell_min_stars**, **title** parameters in [starGift](/constructor/starGift.md)
* Added **backdrop_id** parameter in [starGiftAttributeBackdrop](/constructor/starGiftAttributeBackdrop.md)
* Added **resell_stars** parameter in [starGiftUnique](/constructor/starGiftUnique.md)
* Added **resale_stars**, **can_transfer_at**, **can_resell_at** parameters in [messageActionStarGiftUnique](/constructor/messageActionStarGiftUnique.md)
* Added **can_transfer_at**, **can_resell_at** parameters in [savedStarGift](/constructor/savedStarGift.md)

#### [Schema](/schema.md)

### [Layer 202](/schema?layer=202)

This layer introduces:

- [End-to-end group calls »](/api/end-to-end/group-calls.md)
    - [Conference deep links »](/api/links.md#conference-links)
- [Service messages for paid message refunds »](/constructor/messageActionPaidMessagesRefunded.md)
- [Service messages for paid message price changes »](/constructor/messageActionPaidMessagesPrice.md)
- Gift privacy settings: 
    - [globalPrivacySettings](/constructor/globalPrivacySettings.md).`disallowed_gifts`/[userFull](/constructor/userFull.md).`disallowed_gifts`: [Disallow the reception of specific gift types »](/constructor/disallowedGiftsSettings.md)
    - [globalPrivacySettings](/constructor/globalPrivacySettings.md).`display_gifts_button`/[userFull](/constructor/userFull.md).`display_gifts_button`: show or hide the gift button in the text field of private chats.
- [Transferring stars from a business account to the business bot » ](/api/stars.md#transferring-stars-from-a-business-account-to-the-business-bot)
- [Sponsored peer search results »](/api/sponsored-messages.md#getting-sponsored-search-results)
- [Business bot rights »](/constructor/businessBotRights.md)
- [Sponsored message methods now don't require specification of the `peer` where the sponsored message is visible »](/api/sponsored-messages.md)
- [Renamed the method used by official apps to check whether an in-store purchase is possible »](/method/payments.canPurchaseStore.md)
- [Paid auth codes for official apps](/constructor/auth.sentCodePaymentRequired.md)

#### [Schema](/schema.md) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [payments.canPurchaseStore](/method/payments.canPurchaseStore.md) - Checks whether a purchase is possible. Must be called before in-store purchase, official apps only.
* Added [contacts.getSponsoredPeers](/method/contacts.getSponsoredPeers.md) - Obtain a list of sponsored peer search results for a given query
* Added [phone.deleteConferenceCallParticipants](/method/phone.deleteConferenceCallParticipants.md) - Remove participants from a [conference call »](/api/end-to-end/group-calls.md#removing-a-participant).
* Added [phone.sendConferenceCallBroadcast](/method/phone.sendConferenceCallBroadcast.md) - Submit a verification message to conference call subchain `1`, see [subchains »](/api/end-to-end/group-calls.md#subchains).
* Added [phone.inviteConferenceCallParticipant](/method/phone.inviteConferenceCallParticipant.md) - Invite a user to a [conference call](/api/group-calls.md#conference-calls).
* Added [phone.declineConferenceCallInvite](/method/phone.declineConferenceCallInvite.md) - Decline a [conference call](/api/group-calls.md#conference-calls) invite.
* Added [phone.getGroupCallChainBlocks](/method/phone.getGroupCallChainBlocks.md) - Fetch blocks from a conference call [subchain »](/api/end-to-end/group-calls.md#subchains); handle the returned [updateGroupCallChainBlocks](/constructor/updateGroupCallChainBlocks.md) as [specified here »](/api/end-to-end/group-calls.md#handling-updates).

##### Changed Methods

* Removed **conference_call** parameter in [phone.requestCall](/method/phone.requestCall.md)
* Added **public_key**, **block** parameters, removed **key_fingerprint** parameter in [phone.joinGroupCall](/method/phone.joinGroupCall.md)
* Added **rights** parameter, removed **can_reply** parameter in [account.updateConnectedBot](/method/account.updateConnectedBot.md)
* Removed **peer** parameter in [messages.viewSponsoredMessage](/method/messages.viewSponsoredMessage.md)
* Removed **peer** parameter in [messages.clickSponsoredMessage](/method/messages.clickSponsoredMessage.md)
* Removed **peer** parameter in [messages.reportSponsoredMessage](/method/messages.reportSponsoredMessage.md)
* Changed type of [phone.createConferenceCall](/method/phone.createConferenceCall.md) from **phone.PhoneCall** to **Updates**
* Added **flags**, **muted**, **video_stopped**, **join**, **random_id**, **public_key**, **block**, **params** parameters, removed **peer**, **key_fingerprint** parameters in [phone.createConferenceCall](/method/phone.createConferenceCall.md)

##### Deleted Methods

* Removed payments.canPurchasePremium

##### New Constructors

* Added [auth.sentCodePaymentRequired](/constructor/auth.sentCodePaymentRequired.md) - Official apps may receive this constructor, indicating that due to the high cost of SMS verification codes for the user's country/provider, the user must purchase a [Telegram Premium](/api/premium.md) subscription in order to proceed with the login/signup, see [here »](/api/auth.md#paid-auth) for more info.
* Added [inputStorePaymentAuthCode](/constructor/inputStorePaymentAuthCode.md) - Indicates payment for a login code.
* Added [updateSentPhoneCode](/constructor/updateSentPhoneCode.md) - A paid login SMS code was successfully sent.
* Added [businessBotRights](/constructor/businessBotRights.md) - [Business bot rights](/api/bots/connected-business-bots.md).
* Added [messageActionPaidMessagesRefunded](/constructor/messageActionPaidMessagesRefunded.md) - Sent from peer A to B, indicates that A refunded all [stars](/api/stars.md) B previously paid to send messages to A, see [here »](/api/paid-messages.md) for more info on paid messages.
* Added [messageActionPaidMessagesPrice](/constructor/messageActionPaidMessagesPrice.md) - The price of [paid messages »](/api/paid-messages.md) in this chat was changed.
* Added [disallowedGiftsSettings](/constructor/disallowedGiftsSettings.md) - Disallow the reception of specific [gift](/api/gifts.md) types.
* Added [sponsoredPeer](/constructor/sponsoredPeer.md) - A sponsored peer.
* Added [contacts.sponsoredPeersEmpty](/constructor/contacts.sponsoredPeersEmpty.md) - There are no sponsored peers for this query.
* Added [contacts.sponsoredPeers](/constructor/contacts.sponsoredPeers.md) - Sponsored peers.
* Added [inputInvoiceBusinessBotTransferStars](/constructor/inputInvoiceBusinessBotTransferStars.md) - Transfer stars from the balance of a user account [connected to a business bot](/api/bots/connected-business-bots.md), to the balance of the [business bot](/api/bots/connected-business-bots.md), see [here »](/api/stars.md#transferring-stars-from-a-business-account-to-the-business-bot) for more info on the full flow.
* Added [inputGroupCallSlug](/constructor/inputGroupCallSlug.md) - Identify a [conference call »](/api/group-calls.md#conference-calls) using the slug from its invitation link.
* Added [inputGroupCallInviteMessage](/constructor/inputGroupCallInviteMessage.md) - Identifies a [conference call »](/api/group-calls.md#conference-calls) using its [messageActionConferenceCall](/constructor/messageActionConferenceCall.md) invitation service message.
* Added [updateGroupCallChainBlocks](/constructor/updateGroupCallChainBlocks.md) - Contains conference call blockchain blocks, see [handling E2E group call updates »](/api/end-to-end/group-calls.md#handling-updates).
* Added [messageActionConferenceCall](/constructor/messageActionConferenceCall.md) - Represents a [conference call »](/api/group-calls.md#conference-calls), or an invitation to one if neither the `missed` nor `active` flags are set.
* Added [phoneCallDiscardReasonMigrateConferenceCall](/constructor/phoneCallDiscardReasonMigrateConferenceCall.md) - This phone call was migrated to a [conference call](/api/end-to-end/group-calls.md).

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **display_gifts_button**, **disallowed_gifts** parameters in [userFull](/constructor/userFull.md)
* Removed **conference_call** parameter in [phoneCallWaiting](/constructor/phoneCallWaiting.md)
* Removed **conference_call** parameter in [phoneCallRequested](/constructor/phoneCallRequested.md)
* Removed **conference_call** parameter in [phoneCallAccepted](/constructor/phoneCallAccepted.md)
* Added **conference_supported** parameter, removed **conference_call** parameter in [phoneCall](/constructor/phoneCall.md)
* Removed **conference_call** parameter in [phoneCallDiscarded](/constructor/phoneCallDiscarded.md)
* Added **display_gifts_button**, **disallowed_gifts** parameters in [globalPrivacySettings](/constructor/globalPrivacySettings.md)
* Added **conference**, **creator**, **invite_link** parameters, removed **conference_from_call** parameter in [groupCall](/constructor/groupCall.md)
* Added **rights** parameter, removed **can_reply** parameter in [connectedBot](/constructor/connectedBot.md)
* Added **rights** parameter, removed **can_reply** parameter in [botBusinessConnection](/constructor/botBusinessConnection.md)

#### PUSH notification changes

##### New PUSH notifications

* Added [CONF_CALL_MISSED](/api/push-updates.md#conf-call-missed) - `You missed a call from {1}`

### [Layer 200](/schema?layer=200)

This layer introduces:

- [Detailed account information for new private chats »](/api/action-bar.md#account-information)
- [Paid messages »](/api/paid-messages.md)
- [Pinning a specific received gift to the profile »](/api/gifts.md#pinning-a-received-gift)
- [Sending paid reactions as a channel »](/api/reactions.md#paid-reaction-privacy)
- [Added a `paid_messages_available` flag to channelFull, indicating whether paid messages can be enabled in this supergroup](/constructor/channelFull.md)
- [Gift a Telegram Premium subscription, paying with Telegram Stars »](/api/premium.md#gifting-telegram-premium)  
    This change also simplifies the Telegram Premium gift flow, fully replacing the [userFull](/constructor/userFull.md).`premium_gifts` flag with [payments.getPremiumGiftCodeOptions](/method/payments.getPremiumGiftCodeOptions.md).
- [Blockchain addresses for collectible gifts moved to the TON blockchain as NFTs »](/api/gifts.md#withdraw-a-collectible-gift-to-the-ton-blockchain)
- All flags added to [channel](/constructor/channel.md) (listed below) are valid `min` flags, meaning they must be applied over the locally stored version even if the `min` flag of the incoming [channel](/constructor/channel.md) is set.
- All flags added to [user](/constructor/user.md) (listed below) are valid `min` flags, meaning they must be applied over the locally stored version even if the `min` flag of the incoming [user](/constructor/user.md) is set.

#### [Schema](/schema.md) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [invokeWithReCaptcha](/method/invokeWithReCaptcha.md) - Official clients only: re-execute a method call that required reCAPTCHA verification via a `RECAPTCHA_CHECK_%s__%s`, where the first placeholder is the `action`, and the second one is the reCAPTCHA key ID.
* Added [account.addNoPaidMessagesException](/method/account.addNoPaidMessagesException.md)
* Added [account.getPaidMessagesRevenue](/method/account.getPaidMessagesRevenue.md) - Get the number of stars we have received from the specified user thanks to [paid messages »](/api/paid-messages.md); the received amount will be equal to the sent amount multiplied by [stars\_paid\_message\_commission\_permille](/api/config.md#stars-paid-message-commission-permille) divided by 1000.
* Added [channels.updatePaidMessagesPrice](/method/channels.updatePaidMessagesPrice.md) - Enable or disable [paid messages »](/api/paid-messages.md) in this [supergroup](/api/channel.md) or [monoforum](/api/monoforum.md).
* Added [users.getRequirementsToContact](/method/users.getRequirementsToContact.md) - Check whether we can write to the specified users, used to implement bulk checks for [Premium-only messages »](/api/privacy.md#require-premium-for-new-non-contact-users) and [paid messages »](/api/paid-messages.md).
* Added [payments.toggleStarGiftsPinnedToTop](/method/payments.toggleStarGiftsPinnedToTop.md) - Pins a received gift on top of the profile of the user or owned channels by using [payments.toggleStarGiftsPinnedToTop](/method/payments.toggleStarGiftsPinnedToTop.md).

##### Changed Methods

* Added **allow_paid_stars** parameter in [messages.sendMessage](/method/messages.sendMessage.md)
* Added **allow_paid_stars** parameter in [messages.sendMedia](/method/messages.sendMedia.md)
* Added **allow_paid_stars** parameter in [messages.forwardMessages](/method/messages.forwardMessages.md)
* Added **allow_paid_stars** parameter in [messages.sendInlineBotResult](/method/messages.sendInlineBotResult.md)
* Added **allow_paid_stars** parameter in [messages.sendMultiMedia](/method/messages.sendMultiMedia.md)
* Added **flags**, **for_paid_reactions** parameters in [channels.getSendAs](/method/channels.getSendAs.md)
* Changed type of **private** from **flags.0?Bool** to **flags.0?PaidReactionPrivacy** in [messages.sendPaidReaction](/method/messages.sendPaidReaction.md)
* Changed type of **private** from **Bool** to **PaidReactionPrivacy** in [messages.togglePaidReactionPrivacy](/method/messages.togglePaidReactionPrivacy.md)

##### Deleted Methods

* Removed users.getIsPremiumRequiredToContact

##### New Constructors

* Added [paidReactionPrivacyDefault](/constructor/paidReactionPrivacyDefault.md) - Uses the default reaction privacy, set using [messages.togglePaidReactionPrivacy](/method/messages.togglePaidReactionPrivacy.md).
* Added [paidReactionPrivacyAnonymous](/constructor/paidReactionPrivacyAnonymous.md) - Send paid reactions anonymously.
* Added [paidReactionPrivacyPeer](/constructor/paidReactionPrivacyPeer.md) - Send paid reactions as the specified peer, fetched using [channels.getSendAs](/method/channels.getSendAs.md) with the `for_paid_reactions` flag set.
* Added [inputPrivacyKeyNoPaidMessages](/constructor/inputPrivacyKeyNoPaidMessages.md) - Who can send you messages without paying, if [paid messages »](/api/paid-messages.md) are enabled.
* Added [privacyKeyNoPaidMessages](/constructor/privacyKeyNoPaidMessages.md) - Who can send you messages without paying, if [paid messages »](/api/paid-messages.md) are enabled.
* Added [account.paidMessagesRevenue](/constructor/account.paidMessagesRevenue.md) - Total number of non-refunded [Telegram Stars](/api/stars.md) a user has spent on sending us messages either directly or through a channel, see [here »](/api/paid-messages.md) for more info on paid messages.
* Added [requirementToContactEmpty](/constructor/requirementToContactEmpty.md) - This user can be freely contacted.
* Added [requirementToContactPremium](/constructor/requirementToContactPremium.md) - This user requires us to buy a [Premium](/api/premium.md) subscription in order to contact them.
* Added [requirementToContactPaidMessages](/constructor/requirementToContactPaidMessages.md) - This user requires us to pay the specified amount of [Telegram Stars](/api/stars.md) to send them a message, see [here »](/api/paid-messages.md) for the full flow.
* Added [inputInvoicePremiumGiftStars](/constructor/inputInvoicePremiumGiftStars.md) - Used to gift a [Telegram Premium](/api/premium.md) subscription to another user, paying with [Telegram Stars](/api/stars.md).

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **paid_message_stars** parameter in [message](/constructor/message.md)
* Added **charge_paid_message_stars**, **registration_month**, **phone_country**, **name_change_date**, **photo_change_date** parameters in [peerSettings](/constructor/peerSettings.md)
* Added **send_paid_messages_stars** parameter, removed **premium_gifts** parameter in [userFull](/constructor/userFull.md)
* Added **send_paid_messages_stars** parameter in [user](/constructor/user.md)
* Added **send_paid_messages_stars** parameter in [channel](/constructor/channel.md)
* Added **paid_messages_available** parameter in [channelFull](/constructor/channelFull.md)
* Added **noncontact_peers_paid_stars** parameter in [globalPrivacySettings](/constructor/globalPrivacySettings.md)
* Added **business_transfer**, **stargift_resale**, **paid_messages**, **premium_gift_months** parameters in [starsTransaction](/constructor/starsTransaction.md)
* Changed type of **private** from **Bool** to **PaidReactionPrivacy** in [updatePaidReactionPrivacy](/constructor/updatePaidReactionPrivacy.md)
* Added **gift_address** parameter in [starGiftUnique](/constructor/starGiftUnique.md)

##### Deleted Constructors

* Removed premiumGiftOption

### [Layer 198](/schema?layer=198)

This layer introduces:

- [Collectibles as emoji statuses (and minor tweaks to the emoji status API) »](/api/emoji-status.md#collectibles-as-emoji-statuses)
- Send gifts to channels
- [Notifications for gifts received by channels »](/api/gifts.md#notifications-for-received-channel-gifts)
- [Get all gifts received by a peer »](/api/gifts.md#list-all-received-gifts)
- [Fetch info about specific gifts owned by a peer we control »](/api/gifts.md#list-specific-owned-gifts)
- [Withdraw a collectible gift to the TON blockchain »](/api/gifts.md#withdraw-a-collectible-gift-to-the-ton-blockchain)
- [Custom video covers »](/api/files.md#video-covers)
- [Custom starting timestamps for videos »](/api/files.md#start-video-at-timestamp)

#### [Schema](/schema.md) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [account.getCollectibleEmojiStatuses](/method/account.getCollectibleEmojiStatuses.md) - Obtain a list of [emoji statuses »](/api/emoji-status.md) for owned or [hosted collectible gifts »](/api/gifts.md#hosted-collectible-gifts).
* Added [payments.getSavedStarGifts](/method/payments.getSavedStarGifts.md) - Fetch the full list of [gifts »](/api/gifts.md#list-all-received-gifts) owned, received or [hosted »](/api/gifts.md#hosted-collectible-gifts) by a peer.
* Added [payments.getSavedStarGift](/method/payments.getSavedStarGift.md) - Fetch info about specific [gifts](/api/gifts.md) owned by a peer we control.
* Added [payments.getStarGiftWithdrawalUrl](/method/payments.getStarGiftWithdrawalUrl.md) - Convert a [collectible gift »](/api/gifts.md) to an NFT on the TON blockchain.
* Added [payments.toggleChatStarGiftNotifications](/method/payments.toggleChatStarGiftNotifications.md) - Enables or disables the reception of notifications every time a [gift »](/api/gifts.md) is received by the specified channel, can only be invoked by admins with `post_messages` [admin rights](/constructor/chatAdminRights.md).

##### Changed Methods

* Added **video_timestamp** parameter in [messages.forwardMessages](/method/messages.forwardMessages.md)
* Added **stargift** parameter, removed **msg_id** parameter in [payments.saveStarGift](/method/payments.saveStarGift.md)
* Added **stargift** parameter, removed **msg_id** parameter in [payments.convertStarGift](/method/payments.convertStarGift.md)
* Added **stargift** parameter, removed **msg_id** parameter in [payments.upgradeStarGift](/method/payments.upgradeStarGift.md)
* Added **stargift** parameter, removed **msg_id** parameter, changed type of **to_id** from **InputUser** to **InputPeer** in [payments.transferStarGift](/method/payments.transferStarGift.md)

##### Deleted Methods

* Removed payments.getUserStarGifts
* Removed payments.getUserStarGift

##### New Constructors

* Added [emojiStatusCollectible](/constructor/emojiStatusCollectible.md) - An owned or [hosted collectible gift »](/api/gifts.md#hosted-collectible-gifts) as emoji status.
* Added [inputEmojiStatusCollectible](/constructor/inputEmojiStatusCollectible.md) - An owned or [hosted collectible gift »](/api/gifts.md#hosted-collectible-gifts) as emoji status: can only be used in [account.updateEmojiStatus](/method/account.updateEmojiStatus.md), is never returned by the API.
* Added [savedStarGift](/constructor/savedStarGift.md) - Represents a [gift](/api/gifts.md) owned by a peer.
* Added [payments.savedStarGifts](/constructor/payments.savedStarGifts.md) - Represents a list of [gifts](/api/gifts.md).
* Added [inputSavedStarGiftUser](/constructor/inputSavedStarGiftUser.md) - A gift received in a private chat with another user.
* Added [inputSavedStarGiftChat](/constructor/inputSavedStarGiftChat.md) - A gift received by a channel we own.
* Added [payments.starGiftWithdrawalUrl](/constructor/payments.starGiftWithdrawalUrl.md) - A URL that can be used to import the exported NFT on Fragment.

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **video_cover**, **video_timestamp** parameters in [inputMediaUploadedDocument](/constructor/inputMediaUploadedDocument.md)
* Added **video_cover**, **video_timestamp** parameters in [inputMediaDocument](/constructor/inputMediaDocument.md)
* Added **video_cover**, **video_timestamp** parameters in [messageMediaDocument](/constructor/messageMediaDocument.md)
* Added **stargifts_available**, **stargifts_count** parameters in [channelFull](/constructor/channelFull.md)
* Added **video_cover**, **video_timestamp** parameters in [inputMediaDocumentExternal](/constructor/inputMediaDocumentExternal.md)
* Added **flags**, **until** parameters in [emojiStatus](/constructor/emojiStatus.md)
* Added **peer** parameter, removed **user_id** parameter in [inputInvoiceStarGift](/constructor/inputInvoiceStarGift.md)
* Added **from_id**, **peer**, **saved_id** parameters in [messageActionStarGift](/constructor/messageActionStarGift.md)
* Changed type of **sender_id** from **flags.0?long** to **flags.0?Peer**, **recipient_id** from **long** to **Peer** in [starGiftAttributeOriginalDetails](/constructor/starGiftAttributeOriginalDetails.md)
* Added **owner_address** parameter, changed type of **owner_id** from **flags.0?long** to **flags.0?Peer** in [starGiftUnique](/constructor/starGiftUnique.md)
* Added **from_id**, **peer**, **saved_id** parameters in [messageActionStarGiftUnique](/constructor/messageActionStarGiftUnique.md)
* Added **stargift** parameter, removed **msg_id** parameter in [inputInvoiceStarGiftUpgrade](/constructor/inputInvoiceStarGiftUpgrade.md)
* Added **stargift** parameter, removed **msg_id** parameter, changed type of **to_id** from **InputUser** to **InputPeer** in [inputInvoiceStarGiftTransfer](/constructor/inputInvoiceStarGiftTransfer.md)

##### Deleted Constructors

* Removed emojiStatusUntil
* Removed userStarGift
* Removed payments.userStarGifts

### [Layer 197](/schema?layer=197)

This layer introduces:

- [Collectible gift links »](/api/links.md#collectible-gift-link)
- [Collectible gift story media areas »](/api/stories.md#collectible-gifts)
- [Collectible webpage previews »](/constructor/webPageAttributeUniqueStarGift.md)
- [Similar bot recommendations »](/api/recommend.md#similar-bots)

#### [Schema](/schema.md) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [bots.getBotRecommendations](/method/bots.getBotRecommendations.md) - Obtain a list of similarly themed bots, selected based on similarities in their subscriber bases, see [here »](/api/recommend.md) for more info.
* Added [payments.getUniqueStarGift](/method/payments.getUniqueStarGift.md) - Obtain info about a [collectible gift »](/api/gifts.md#collectible-gifts) using a slug obtained from a [collectible gift link »](/api/links.md#collectible-gift-link).

##### Changed Methods

* Changed type of [messages.getWebPagePreview](/method/messages.getWebPagePreview.md) from **MessageMedia** to **messages.WebPagePreview**

##### New Constructors

* Added [users.users](/constructor/users.users.md) - Describes a list of users (or bots).
* Added [users.usersSlice](/constructor/users.usersSlice.md) - Describes a partial list of users.
* Added [payments.uniqueStarGift](/constructor/payments.uniqueStarGift.md) - Represents a [collectible gift »](/api/gifts.md#collectible-gifts).
* Added [webPageAttributeUniqueStarGift](/constructor/webPageAttributeUniqueStarGift.md) - Contains info about [collectible gift »](/api/gifts.md#collectible-gifts) for a [webPage](/constructor/webPage.md) preview of a [collectible gift »](/api/gifts.md#collectible-gifts) (the [webPage](/constructor/webPage.md) will have a `type` of `telegram_nft`).
* Added [mediaAreaStarGift](/constructor/mediaAreaStarGift.md) - Represents a [collectible gift »](/api/gifts.md#collectible-gifts).
* Added [messages.webPagePreview](/constructor/messages.webPagePreview.md) - Represents a webpage preview.

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **flags**, **slug**, **owner_name** parameters, changed type of **owner_id** from **long** to **flags.0?long** in [starGiftUnique](/constructor/starGiftUnique.md)

#### [Schema](/schema.md)

### [Layer 196](/schema?layer=196)

This layer introduces:

- [Extra secure group calls »](https://telegram.org/blog/group-calls-made-easy)
- [Explicit delivery acknowledgement for Telegram Gateway verification messages »](/method/messages.reportMessagesDelivery.md).
- [Third-party verification »](/api/bots/verification.md)
- [Reactions for service messages »](/api/reactions.md)
- [Collectible gifts »](/api/gifts.md#collectible-gifts)
- Add support for entities in folder titles, and a new `title_noanimate` flag to optionally freeze animated emoji entities in the title.
- All flags added to [channel](/constructor/channel.md) (listed below) are valid `min` flags, meaning they must be applied over the locally stored version even if the `min` flag of the incoming [channel](/constructor/channel.md) is set.
- All flags added to [user](/constructor/user.md) (listed below) are valid `min` flags, meaning they must be applied over the locally stored version even if the `min` flag of the incoming [user](/constructor/user.md) is set.

#### PUSH notification changes

##### New PUSH notifications

* Added [MESSAGE_STARGIFT_UPGRADE](/api/push-updates.md#message-stargift-upgrade) - `{1} upgraded your Gift`
* Added [MESSAGE_UNIQUE_STARGIFT](/api/push-updates.md#message-unique-stargift) - `{1} transferred you a Gift!`
